### [69] [Extending Music Annotation Schemas: Zero-Shot Prediction or Few-Shot Adaptation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06920)
*Christos Plachouras,Emmanouil Benetos,Johan Pauwels*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 论文构建基于MGPHot的基准以模拟音乐标注模式扩展，比较零样本预测、预训练表征学习与监督适配，发现在小预算下监督适配已优于零样本，中等预算下冻结表征复用最有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业音乐目录需随需求演变不断新增音乐属性，而专家标注成本高昂。现有自动音乐标注通常假设标注模式固定，面对新模式扩展与旧曲目回填时，何种方法最有效、零样本音频-语言模型相比监督适配在多大标注预算下更具优势尚不明确。

Method: 提出基于MGPHot流行音乐标注数据集的基准，模拟不同标注预算下的音乐模式扩展，并系统比较三类方法：音频-语言模型的零样本预测、从预训练表征学习新属性、以及基于已有标注训练模型的适配。

Result: 监督适配即使在小标注预算下也比零样本预测更有效；在中等预算下，冻结表征复用无需深度适配所需的调优，仍是最有效的方法。

Conclusion: 音频-语言模型的零样本能力在音乐标注模式扩展中并不占优；实践中应优先考虑监督适配，并在预算有限时采用冻结表征复用策略。

Abstract: Automatic music annotation is typically tackled under the assumption of a fixed annotation schema. In practice, commercial music catalogs often need to accommodate new musical attributes as needs evolve. Given that expert music annotation is expensive, it is not evident which methodological approach is most effective at accommodating new attributes and backfilling existing tracks; audio-language models promise zero-shot prediction, but at what annotation budget does supervised adaptation become more compelling?
  We propose a benchmark based on the MGPHot popular music annotation dataset for simulating music schema extension across different annotation budgets. We investigate zero-shot prediction with audio-language models, learning new attributes from pretrained representations, and adapting models trained on existing annotations. Our results suggest that supervised adaptation is more effective than zero-shot prediction even with small annotation budgets, while frozen representation reuse remains the most effective approach for modest budgets without the tuning required by deeper adaptation.

</details>


### [70] [AdaLoop: Adaptive-Depth Latent Reasoning for Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06949)
*Lee Seung-woo,Bowen Qi*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: AdaLoop是一个轻量级循环模块，通过自适应调整潜在精炼步数，提升大型音频语言模型在细粒度声学分析任务上的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频语言模型在语音、声音和音乐问答上表现良好，但在需要细粒度声学分析的任务上准确率急剧下降。此类任务（如判断两位说话者谁音调更高）需要迭代的、信号层面的推理，而当前模型对内容型问题和声学推理问题分配相同的计算深度，导致效率与效果不匹配。

Method: 提出AdaLoop，一个轻量级循环模块，学习给定音频-问题对所需的潜在精炼步数。其核心是一个共享transformer块，在问题的引导下对音频表示进行迭代；并配有可学习的停机机制，当表示准备就绪时退出循环。该模块仅增加不到基础模型3%的参数，可插入任意音频编码器-语言模型组合，且不修改原有组件。

Result: 在三种架构不同的模型上，于MMSU、MMAU-Pro和MMAR三个基准进行评估，AdaLoop将平均准确率提升2.9至3.8个百分点，且在感知密集型子任务上增益最大，表明模型学会了应用更深的推理。

Conclusion: AdaLoop以极小的参数开销实现自适应计算深度分配，有效提升音频语言模型在细粒度感知任务上的性能，具有良好的通用性和可移植性。

Abstract: Large audio language models answer questions about speech, sound, and music, yet their accuracy drops sharply on tasks that need fine-grained acoustic analysis. Judging which of two speakers has the higher pitch demands iterative signal-level reasoning that a content question does not. Current models spend the same computational depth on both. We introduce AdaLoop, a lightweight recurrent module that learns how many latent refinement steps a given audio--question pair requires. A shared transformer block iterates over the audio representation, guided by the question, while a learned halting mechanism exits the loop once the representation is ready. AdaLoop adds fewer than 3\% of the base model's parameters and plugs into any audio encoder--language model pair without modifying either component. Evaluated on three architecturally distinct models across MMSU, MMAU-Pro, and MMAR, AdaLoop raises the average accuracy by 2.9 to 3.8 points, with the largest gains on perception-heavy subtasks where the model learns to apply deeper reasoning.

</details>


### [71] [Where Does the Audio Jailbreak Live? A Controlled Frequency-Depth Audit of AdvWave-P on Qwen2-Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07005)
*Boyuan Chen,Minseok Kim,Sohaila Abdulsattar,Minghao Shao,Siddharth Garg,Ramesh Karri,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文对音频越狱方法AdvWave-P在Qwen2-Audio上的频率与解码深度主张进行审计，发现其频率排序高度依赖频带划分方式，细分辨率下的顶频带结果无法解释，且模型内部的音频跨度发散与频带必要性存在关联但非因果定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新检验AdvWave-P这种加性音频越狱方法在Qwen2-Audio上的频率成分与解码层深度相关主张，特别是其攻击成功是否真的依赖于特定频带，以及这些主张在多大程度上受实验设置（如频带划分、评判方式）影响。

Method: 采用STFT域掩蔽协议，在520条AdvBench提示上测量攻击成功率与音频跨度表征；使用条件盲单标注者验证并基于Rogan-Gladen估计灵敏度；比较等Hz与等能量划分及8频带/16频带等能量分辨率；进行匹配能量散布移除测试、全局重缩放、重优化试点（n=20）以及提示与能量调整模型中各层音频跨度发散与频带必要性的关联分析。

Result: 主评判将76.7%的对抗输入标为越狱；验证灵敏度约0.87（0.83-0.95），未对掩蔽条件校正。能量占比可预测8频带排序（Spearman rho=0.95）。在16频带等能量分辨率下，掩蔽窄频带7520-7960 Hz后ASR仍为0.10且无匹配对照无法解释。匹配能量连续移除在低频伤害更大，高频两者均接近下限。全局重缩放仅检验幅度敏感性。重优化试点中单/双频带支撑ASR为0.00、0.25、0.40，而随机支撑约覆盖一半STFT bin时均值达0.86。音频跨度发散与频带必要性关联，系数从投影器输出+0.63升至第30层+0.93（对比+0.293，95% CI [0.11, 0.51]）。

Conclusion: 结果支持对频率主张进行划分感知审计，细分辨率顶频带结果与更广泛泛化性仍待解决；音频跨度发散与频带必要性的关联并非因果定位，单层修补也不能确立因果层。

Abstract: We audit frequency and decoder-depth claims for AdvWave-P, an additive audio jailbreak, on Qwen2-Audio. The protocol masks frequency components of the perturbation in the short-time Fourier transform (STFT) domain and measures attack success and audio-span representations. On 520 AdvBench prompts, the primary judge labels 76.7% of adversarial inputs as jailbreaks. A condition-blind, single-annotator validation yields a Rogan-Gladen sensitivity estimate of 0.87 for this condition (about 0.83-0.95 with validation-rate uncertainty); this correction is not applied to masked conditions. The apparent frequency ranking depends on the partition: energy share alone predicts the standard eight-band ranking (Spearman's rho = 0.95), and equal-Hz and equal-energy partitions show that masking any tested band can sharply reduce attack success. At a finer 16-band equal-energy resolution, however, masking the narrow 7520-7960 Hz band leaves ASR at 0.10, which remains unresolved without a matched control. Matched-energy scattered-removal tests show that contiguous removal is more damaging in lower bands, while both forms approach the floor in upper bands. Global rescaling leaves ASR near baseline but tests amplitude sensitivity rather than frequency location. In a re-optimization pilot (n = 20), tested single- and two-band supports reach ASRs of 0.00, 0.25, and 0.40, while random supports covering about half the STFT bins reach a mean of 0.86. In a prompt- and energy-adjusted model, audio-span divergence is associated with band necessity, with the coefficient rising from +0.63 at the projector output to +0.93 at layer 30 (contrast +0.293, 95% CI [0.11, 0.51]). This association is not a causal localization, and single-layer patching does not establish a causal layer. The results support partition-aware auditing of frequency claims, leaving the fine-resolution top-band result and broader generality open.

</details>


### [72] [ImpactMat: Continuous Material Estimation for Inverse Impact Sound Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07061)
*Hyebin Cho,Bumsoo Kim,Joon son Chung*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出逆撞击声渲染任务：从参考撞击声中预测材质参数，以使模拟器重现相似的材料响应；并发布ImpactMat数据集与基准，以及一个前馈预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有撞击声渲染器依赖木材、塑料、钢等固定材质预设，限制了可表达的撞击声范围；而手动调整底层材质参数需要材料声学专业知识，门槛较高。因此需要从声音反推材质参数的方法。

Method: 提出逆撞击声渲染任务；构建ImpactMat数据集与基准，包含单一材质与混合材质的撞击声及其对应的真实材质参数；提出一个前馈模型，从一段或多段录音中预测材质参数，并利用混合材质学习不同材质类型之间的平滑过渡。

Result: 实验表明，所提方法优于多个竞争性基线，并能在无需手动调参的情况下，从真实录音实现重新渲染。

Conclusion: 该工作为基于声音的材质参数预测提供了数据集、基准与有效方法，使撞击声渲染摆脱固定预设与专业调参的限制。

Abstract: Impact sound rendering synthesizes the sound produced when a 3D object is struck, but practical renderers often rely on fixed material presets such as wood, plastic, or steel. These presets limit the range of impact sounds a renderer can express, while manually adjusting the underlying material parameters remains difficult without expertise in material acoustics. We therefore study inverse impact sound rendering: predicting material parameters from a reference impact sound so that a simulator can recreate a similar material response. To support this task, we introduce ImpactMat, a dataset and benchmark of single and blended material impact sounds paired with ground-truth material parameters. We further propose a feed-forward model that predicts these parameters from one or more recordings, using blended materials to learn smooth transitions between material types. Experiments show that our method outperforms competitive baselines and enables re-rendering from real recordings without manual parameter tuning. The project page is available https://material-from-impact.github.io/material-from-impact/.

</details>


### [73] [Neural Representations, Natural Connections: What Transfers From Human Speech Foundation Models to Animal Vocalizations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07107)
*Tomás Arias-Vergara,Christopher Hauer,Héloïse Brotier,Elmar Nöth,Andreas Maier,Lee Koren*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 系统评测15个冻结预训练编码器在跨物种动物声学任务上的迁移表现，发现语音与动物预训练表征差距有限，且迁移效果强烈依赖网络层选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音预训练模型在动物生物声学中展现出潜力，但决定其跨物种迁移效果的关键因素尚不清楚，缺乏对语言覆盖度、动物域预训练、模型类型等变量的系统理解。

Method: 评测15个冻结编码器，涵盖单语/多语语音、说话人验证、动物生物声学与通用音频预训练模型；在4个物种的鸣叫者识别与3个物种的呼叫类型分类任务上进行线性探测，并通过受控比较分析语言覆盖度、动物域预训练及层深对迁移的影响。

Result: 最强语音与动物预训练表征的UAR差值在-0.027至+0.119之间；7个设定中语音在3个上显著更优（p<0.01），其余4个无显著差异。增加语言覆盖度或动物域预训练未带来系统性优势，冻结说话人验证嵌入迁移效果较差；迁移呈强层依赖：原始波形模型在浅层达峰，而块状频谱图模型在更深层达峰。

Conclusion: 语音预训练表征可作为动物生物声学的有效通用特征，跨物种迁移不依赖语言覆盖度或动物域数据；性能主要受编码器架构与层选择调控，需针对模型类型选择最优层。

Abstract: Speech-pretrained models have shown promise in animal bioacoustics, but the factors governing their cross-species transfer remain poorly understood. We evaluate 15 frozen encoders spanning monolingual and multilingual speech, speaker verification, animal bioacoustics, and general audio on caller identification across four species and call-type classification across three. Differences between the strongest speech- and animal-pretrained representations range from $-0.027$ to $+0.119$ UAR; speech is significantly better in three of seven settings ($p<0.01$) and not significantly different in the remaining four. Controlled comparisons show no systematic advantage from increased language coverage or animal-domain pretraining, while frozen speaker-verification embeddings transfer poorly. The results also show that transfer is strongly layer-dependent: raw-waveform models peak early, whereas patch-spectrogram models peak deeper.

</details>


### [74] [Exposing and Mitigating Neural Codec Vulnerabilities in Audio Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07216)
*Abdullah,Awais Khan,Khalid Mahmood Malik*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文揭示现有音频深伪检测（ADD）模型对神经音频编解码器压缩的鲁棒性严重不足，构建了 Audio Neural Codec-Spoof 数据集，并提出 PCL-NET 方法，将神经编解码压缩下的平均 EER 从 28.67% 降至 12.77%，同时保持竞争性的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ADD 数据集与检测器主要针对基于声码器的合成，仅在生成过程之外独立地评估 MP3/AAC 压缩、加性噪声等事后扰动。而近期语音合成器（尤其是基于 ALM 的系统）将神经音频编解码器同时用于压缩和作为从生成 token 重建波形的重合成组件，产生的伪影与事后压缩截然不同。神经编解码器因此具有双重角色：一部分面向低带宽通信的纯压缩，另一部分作为重合成组件。然而，与事后压缩不同，对基于编解码器压缩的鲁棒性研究仍基本空白。作者指出现有 SOTA 模型在编解码压缩语音上性能急剧下降，尤其是以 Codec Resynthesized 数据作为代理训练的系统最脆弱，常将合法压缩的真实语音误判为伪造。

Method: 1）构建 Audio Neural Codec-Spoof 数据集：将七种神经编解码算法应用于现有 ADD 基准，把编解码器引入的重合成伪影作为编解码生成的可控代理，以隔离该因素。2）提出 PCL-NET（Pairwise Consistency Learned Network）：微调预训练 XLS-R（300M）编码器，采用成对一致性目标，最小化同一语音未压缩版本与编解码压缩版本之间的表征距离，从而将编解码伪影与真伪判别解耦。3）作为基线缓解方案，并与 CoSG 等检测性能进行对比评估。

Result: PCL-NET 将神经编解码压缩下的平均 EER 从 28.67% 降至 12.77%，同时保持具有竞争力的基于 CoSG 的深伪检测性能。数据集在论文接收后将于 Hugging Face 公开。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了 ADD 领域对神经编解码压缩鲁棒性研究的空白，证明现有模型（尤其以 Codec Resynthesized 数据训练者）在该场景下严重失效。所提出的 Audio Neural Codec-Spoof 数据集与 PCL-NET 方法有效缓解了该问题，为编解码压缩场景下的深伪检测提供了可控代理与基线方案。

Abstract: Existing audio deepfake detection (ADD) datasets and detectors are primarily built for vocoder-based synthesis, evaluated against traditional post-hoc perturbations such as MP3/AAC compression or additive noise, applied independently of generation. However, recent speech synthesizers, particularly ALM-based systems, use neural audio codecs both for compression and as the resynthesis reconstructing waveforms from generated tokens, producing artifacts distinct from post-hoc compression. Neural codecs thus play a dual role: some are designed for pure compression under low-bandwidth communication, while others serve as resynthesis components. Despite this dual role, robustness to codec-based compression, unlike post-hoc compression, remains largely unexplored. We expose this gap, showing that state-of-the-art (SOTA) ADD models degrade drastically on codec-compressed speech; in particular, systems trained on Codec Resynthesized data as a proxy for codec-based generation prove most vulnerable, with legitimately compressed bona fide speech often misclassified as fake. To investigate this, we construct the Audio Neural Codec-Spoof dataset by applying seven neural codec algorithms to existing ADD benchmarks, isolating codec-induced resynthesis artifacts as a controlled proxy for codec-based generation. As baseline mitigation, we propose PCL-NET (Pairwise Consistency Learned Network), fine-tuning a pretrained XLS-R (300M) encoder with a pairwise consistency objective that minimizes the representation distance between an utterance's uncompressed and codec-compressed versions, disentangling codec artifacts from the real-versus-fake decision. As a result, PCL-NET reduces average EER under neural codec compression from 28.67% to 12.77%, while preserving competitive CoSG-based deepfake detection performance. We will also make the dataset publicly available on Hugging Face upon acceptance.

</details>


### [75] [Adapting a Latent Audio Diffusion Model to Historical Guqin Recordings: A Listening-Driven Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07486)
*Huanchen Cai*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 将预训练潜空间音频扩散模型（Stable Audio 3 Medium）通过 r16 DoRA 适配器微调至古琴风格，构建可连续播放的"AI 电台"，并报告了正负两方面的探索性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是构建一个能无休止播放古琴风格音乐的"AI 电台"，但古琴领域可用数据规模小（仅412段独奏录音），需要验证小数据条件下适配预训练潜空间扩散模型的可行性，并记录哪些做法有效、哪些无效。

Method: 从历史录音中整理412段独奏（42.5小时、61位演奏者），按曲目划分数据集；在 Stable Audio 3 Medium 的连续潜空间上微调 rank-16 DoRA 适配器；采用偏向"续写"的 loss 加权、结合曲目研究笔记与情绪标签及自动估计五声音阶调式的 caption、随机长度裁剪，在留出损失不再改善时停止训练；由一位专家听者进行七次盲听研究指导全部决策。

Result: 最终适配器在续写未见曲目上评分最高（3.9/5，对比此前最佳适配器 3.4/5），在依自由写作场景描述生成上达 4.6/5。负结果包括：基于相同潜空间从头训练的 AR 模型无法产生可辨识音色；信号级摩擦噪声指标与听者抱怨呈反向相关；五声音阶契合度指标整体与评分相关、但在候选组内几乎无区分度。长时段链接续写暴露了每段生成音频的静音尾巴与响度反馈回路，二者均有简单修复方案。受限于单一听者与每条件最多十条样本，无任何配对差异达到统计显著。

Conclusion: 该工作证明小数据下将预训练潜空间音频扩散模型适配至古琴风格是可行的，且负结果同样具有信息价值；但由于样本量与听者数量有限，结果仅为探索性记录，不构成统计显著结论。

Abstract: We report a small-data case study in adapting a pretrained latent audio diffusion model to the guqin, the seven-string Chinese zither, aiming at an "AI radio" that plays guqin-style music without end. From a library of historical recordings we curate 412 solo performances (42.5 h, 61 performers) and split them by composition. We fine-tune a rank-16 DoRA adapter on Stable Audio 3 Medium using its continuous latents, masking weighted towards continuation, captions that combine researched notes on each piece with mood tags and an automatically estimated pentatonic mode, and random-length crops, stopping when held-out loss stops improving. Seven blind listening studies by one expert listener guided every decision. The final adapter was rated highest for continuing unseen pieces (3.9/5, against 3.4 for the best earlier adapter) and 4.6/5 for generating from free-written scene descriptions. Negative results are equally informative: a from-scratch autoregressive model over the same latents produced no recognisable timbre, a signal-level friction-noise measure correlated with the listener's complaints in the wrong direction, and a pentatonic-fit measure tracked ratings overall but barely within a group of candidates. Chaining continuations for long playback exposed a silent tail on every generated clip and a loudness feedback loop, both with simple fixes. With one listener and at most ten clips per condition, no paired difference is statistically significant; we present an exploratory record of what helped, what did not, and why.

</details>


### [76] [SkillFormer: Skill-Decomposed Adaptation for Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07533)
*Lee Seung-woo,Bowen Qi,Kim Min-jun,Jang Won-young*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出 SkillFormer，用技能专属低秩适配器加可学习路由器实现多技能音频理解，缓解多任务干扰，仅增加<4%参数即在多个基准上提升2.5-4.1分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频语言模型需同时处理音高比较、说话人计数、节奏估计、情感识别等数十种技能，联合训练会产生技能间干扰：某一技能的提升常以另一技能性能下降为代价。

Method: 将音频理解分解为技能专属的低秩适配器（LoRA类），推理时由可学习路由器根据问题决定激活哪些适配器及其权重，实现按查询动态组合参数；采用交替训练策略，先在各技能簇上分别更新对应适配器，再联合校准路由器，从而避免标准多任务优化中的梯度冲突。方法不改动音频编码器与语言主干，新增参数低于基座模型的4%。

Result: 在三种架构不同的模型上，于 MMSU、MMAU-Pro 和 MMAR 三个基准评测，平均准确率提升2.5至4.1分，且在感知、推理与语义各子类别上增益均衡。

Conclusion: 通过技能解耦的低秩适配与路由组合，可在参数高效、不改动主干的前提下有效缓解音频多技能学习中的任务干扰，实现跨模型架构的稳定均衡提升。

Abstract: Audio language models must handle dozens of distinct skills, from pitch comparison and speaker counting to musical tempo estimation and emotion recognition. Joint training on all skills at once causes interference: gains on one skill often come at the cost of another. We propose \textbf{SkillFormer}, which decomposes audio understanding into skill-specific low-rank adapters and composes them at inference time through a learned router. The router examines the question to decide which adapters to activate and how much weight each should carry, so that a pitch query engages different parameters than a genre classification query. An alternating training schedule updates each adapter on its own skill cluster before jointly calibrating the router, preventing the gradient conflicts that arise in standard multi-task optimization. SkillFormer adds fewer than 4\% of the base model's parameters and requires no changes to the audio encoder or language backbone. Evaluated on three architecturally distinct models across MMSU, MMAU-Pro, and MMAR, it raises the average accuracy by 2.5 to 4.1 points, with balanced gains across perception, reasoning, and semantic subcategories.

</details>


### [77] [Pronunciation-Oriented Reinforcement Learning for Japanese Text-to-Speech with Kana-Domain ASR Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07575)
*Shiao Zhu,Lianbo Liu,Kai Washizaki,Koki Nikaido,Yui Sudo*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文针对日语TTS强化学习后训练中的可懂度奖励问题，提出在假名域（kana domain）计算字符错误率（Kana-CER），以消除日语正字法CER带来的发音表征错配，在保持正字法CER、说话人相似度和语音质量相当的情况下，将目标汉字读音错误相对降低约26%，并显著减少优化步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在日语TTS的强化学习后训练中，通常使用ASR计算的正字法CER作为可懂度奖励。但日语存在语音与正字法的不一致：不同汉字读音可能对应相同的正字法表征，而相同发音又可能有不同的正字法形式。这种表征错配导致正字法CER并非面向发音优化的理想奖励信号，因此需要一种更贴合发音的评价方式。

Method: 作者提出在假名域计算CER（Kana-CER）：使用能够转写假名的ASR模型，并结合参考读音（reference readings）计算字符错误率。在匹配的组相对策略优化（GRPO）条件下，将Kana-CER与正字法CER奖励进行对比实验；同时考察了无正则化的Kana-CER优化下的输出问题及KL正则化的作用。

Result: 在匹配的GRPO条件下，Kana-CER相比正字法CER奖励将目标汉字读音错误相对降低约26%，同时保持相当的正字法CER以及相近的说话人相似度和客观语音质量。Kana-CER还在显著更少的优化步数内达到最佳验证性能（4k步 vs 18k步）。此外，无正则化的Kana-CER优化会导致严重的输出拉长，而KL正则化可大幅抑制该现象。

Conclusion: 在日语TTS的RL后训练中，采用假名域的CER作为可懂度奖励比正字法CER更符合发音导向的优化目标，能够在更少优化步数下取得更低的汉字读音错误率，并保持其他指标不退化；同时需借助KL正则化抑制输出拉长问题。

Abstract: Character error rate (CER) computed by automatic speech recognition (ASR) is widely used as an intelligibility reward for reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of text-to-speech (TTS) systems. For Japanese, however, orthographic CER introduces a representation mismatch for pronunciation-oriented optimization: distinct kanji readings may collapse to the same orthographic representation, while equivalent pronunciations may admit different orthographic forms. We instead compute CER in the kana domain using a kana-transcribing ASR model and reference readings (Kana-CER). Under matched group relative policy optimization (GRPO) conditions, Kana-CER reduces target-kanji reading error by approximately 26% relative to the orthographic CER reward, while maintaining comparable orthographic CER and similar speaker similarity and objective speech quality. It also reaches its best validation performance in substantially fewer optimization steps (4k vs. 18k). We further observe severe output elongation under unregularized Kana-CER optimization, which is substantially suppressed by KL regularization.

</details>


### [78] [Hiding Tool Latency in On-Device Cascaded Voice Agent through Speculative Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07641)
*Kyudan Jung,Hyunsin Park,Yoonhyung Lee,Jinhwan Park,Jinhyeok Yang,KiHyun Nam,Jaegul Choo,Jinkyu Lee*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 面向端侧级联语音助手，提出推测性工具执行：从部分ASR假设预测工具调用并提前执行、缓存结果注入LLM提示，将首音频中位时延从5.79s降至4.60s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统级联语音助手将ASR、LLM推理与外部工具执行串行化，工具时延只能在用户说完且LLM识别出工具调用后才产生，导致端到端响应延迟高。作者希望在不改变既有级联架构的前提下，通过在语音尚未结束时提前启动工具执行来降低响应时延。

Method: 提出推测性工具执行框架：1) 引入Predictor模块，基于部分ASR假设在识别过程中预测工具调用，并推测性执行、缓存结果；2) 将缓存输出注入LLM提示以加速响应；3) 针对用户自我纠正，采用基于规则的选择性校验机制，仅注入有效缓存结果；4) 保底机制是LLM仍可直接发起工具调用，使框架最坏情况时延不超过基线串行流水线。

Result: 在完整实现的Android语音助手上进行实测：首音频时延中位数由5.79 s降至4.60 s，标准差由3.49 s降至2.81 s，响应时延更可预测。

Conclusion: 推测性工具执行能在端侧级联语音助手中有效降低并稳定端到端响应时延，且通过规则校验与LLM兜底保证了鲁棒性与时延上界。

Abstract: Tool-augmented speech assistants typically serialize automatic speech recognition, large language model inference, and external tool execution. As a result, tool latency is incurred only after the user has finished speaking and the LLM has identified the required tool calls. We present speculative tool execution for on-device cascaded voice agents, which predicts tool requests from partial ASR hypotheses and initiates tool execution while speech is still being received, thereby reducing end-to-end response latency. Our approach introduces a Predictor module that anticipates tool calls during speech recognition, executes them speculatively, and caches the results. The cached outputs are then injected into the LLM prompt, enabling faster responses. Additionally, to mitigate errors caused by user self-corrections during speech, we employ a rule-based validation mechanism that selectively injects only valid cached results. As a final safeguard, the LLM retains the ability to issue tool calls directly, ensuring that the latency of our framework is upper-bounded by the baseline serial execution pipeline in the worst case. We evaluate our method using live measurements from a fully implemented Android voice assistant. Our approach reduces the median time-to-first-audio from 5.79,s to 4.60,s and decreases the standard deviation from 3.49,s to 2.81,s, resulting in more predictable response latency.

</details>


### [79] [Loud and Clear: Dynamic Activation Steering for Improving Speech Intelligibility in Noisy Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07647)
*Seymanur Akti,Alexander Waibel*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出基于激活引导（activation steering）的免重训练TTS控制方法，模拟Lombard效应提升噪声下语音可懂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在噪声环境中会自然调整发声以提升可懂度（Lombard效应），但预训练TTS模型是否需要重训练才能生成此类语音尚不明确。

Method: 采用激活引导对预训练TTS模型进行推理时控制，聚焦增加发声努力与过度发音两个特征；提出prompt-relative steering机制，避免生成过程中引导效应累积，同时支持动态调整强度。

Result: 在已见与未见说话人及多语言条件下，系统改变Lombard相关声学特征，说话人相似度保持89-95%，1 dB SNR背景噪声下WER降低7-22%。

Conclusion: 预训练TTS模型无需重训练即可被动态控制以生成更可懂语音。

Abstract: Speech becomes less intelligible in noisy environments, and humans naturally adapt their voice to compensate. Inspired by this behavior, we investigate whether a text-to-speech (TTS) model can be guided to produce more intelligible speech using activation steering, without retraining. We focus on two characteristics of the Lombard effect: increased vocal effort and hyper-articulation. We introduce a prompt-relative steering mechanism that prevents steering effects from accumulating during generation while allowing their strength to be adjusted dynamically. Across seen and unseen speakers and multiple languages, our method produces systematic changes in Lombard-related acoustic features, preserves speaker similarity (89-95%), and reduces WER under background noise by 7-22% at 1 dB SNR. These results show that pretrained TTS models can be dynamically controlled to generate more intelligible speech without retraining.

</details>


### [80] [HiPLEX: Hierarchical Policy Factorization for Full Duplex Speech Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07727)
*Kyudan Jung,Hyunsin Park,Yoonhyung Lee,Jinhwan Park,Jinhyeok Yang,KiHyun Nam,Jaegul Choo,Jinkyu Lee*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出 HiPLEX，一个将预训练全双工文本策略分解为"何时说"的控制策略与"说什么"的条件内容策略的强化学习框架，并分别路由时序优势与语义优势，在全双工对话基准上优于 GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人机交互日趋对话化，具备自然实时对话能力的全双工语音语言模型愈发重要。这类模型不仅要生成恰当回复，还须实时协调话轮转换、反馈信号（backchanneling）与话语权管理。强化学习可通过对交互结果的直接反馈来优化这些行为，但现有 RL 方法要么仅把时序反馈施加于 token 策略，要么只优化语义内容，未能联合改善时序与内容。

Method: HiPLEX 将预训练全双工文本策略因式分解为两个因子：控制策略决定何时输出内容，条件内容策略决定输出什么内容。前者在 'pad'、'epad'、'con' 三者间选择；后者仅当选择 'con' 时选出一个 token。该层级结构刻画了每一帧内的条件动作，并复用模型已有的文本头。方法通过由生成语音片段导出的事件因果掩码，将时序优势路由到 token 组因子；同时将 LLM 评审（LLM-judge）得到的语义优势路由到条件内容因子。

Result: 在 Full-Duplex-Bench v1 上，使用三个 Moshi 随机种子，HiPLEX 相较 GRPO 降低了用户自然停顿与反馈机会期间的抢话率（takeover rate），并缩短了被打断后的响应延迟，同时保持相当的被打断回复质量（经评审）。在 Moshi 与 PersonaPlex 上，HiPLEX 比 GRPO 更贴合汇总后的人类话轮时序与反馈率边际分布。

Conclusion: 通过将全双工策略分解为时序控制与条件内容两个因子，并分别路由时序与语义优势信号，HiPLEX 实现了时序与内容的联合优化，在抢话抑制、响应延迟及人类对话统计分布匹配方面优于 GRPO，为全双工语音对话模型的实时交互行为优化提供了有效框架。

Abstract: As human--AI interactions become more conversational, full-duplex speech language models capable of natural real-time dialogue are growing in importance. Beyond generating appropriate responses, these models must coordinate turn-taking, backchanneling, and floor management in real time. Reinforcement learning (RL) provides a way to refine these behaviors through direct feedback on interaction outcomes. However, existing RL methods either apply timing feedback to a token policy or optimize semantic content, leaving the joint improvement of timing and content unresolved. We introduce HiPLEX, an RL framework that factorizes a pretrained full-duplex text policy into a control policy that decides when to emit content and a conditional content policy that decides what to emit. The first factor selects among 'pad', 'epad', and 'con'. The second selects a token only when 'con' is chosen. This hierarchy describes conditional actions within each frame and uses the model's existing text head. We route timing advantages to the token-group factor through event-causal masks derived from generated speech episodes, and route an LLM-judge semantic advantage to the conditional content factor. Across three Moshi seeds on Full-Duplex-Bench v1, HiPLEX reduces takeover rates during natural user pauses and backchannel opportunities, and shortens post-interruption response latency relative to GRPO, while maintaining comparable judged interruption-response quality. On Moshi and PersonaPlex, HiPLEX better matches pooled human turn-timing and backchannel-rate marginals than GRPO.

</details>


### [81] [Feature Encoding in VAE-based Audio Decoders: Effects of Input, Depth and Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07966)
*Louis McCallum,Mick Grierson*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 对RAVE和EnCodec解码器激活进行逐层与跨层聚类分析，揭示神经音频合成模型内部对音高、BPM等音乐特征的编码规律与深度分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经音频合成模型（如RAVE）生成质量高，但其内部表征如何编码音乐特征仍缺乏理解，需要提升模型可解释性并指导神经合成的可控策略。

Method: 对三个不同音乐领域训练的RAVE解码器激活进行系统性的逐层与跨层聚类分析，使用四种刺激类型测试，并以通用EnCodec模型评估架构泛化性；采用线性和非线性探针（probe）分析特征编码强度与联合编码。

Result: 合成刺激编码良好（音高|ρ|=0.45，为基线的5.1倍；BPM|ρ|=0.76，为基线8.6倍）；自然音频编码减弱但仍显著（均值|ρ|=0.25，基线2.8倍）；自然音频非线性探针表现更强（均值R²=0.56，基线18倍，非线性增益+0.152）；编码强度沿解码器层变化，中间层呈现更强的联合编码能力（β2均为负，p<0.05）；EnCodec表现类似；最佳跨层聚类提升BPM编码强度（r=0.65，p=0.006）和普遍性（r=0.75，p=0.001），对联合编码无影响。

Conclusion: 研究推进了神经音频模型的可解释性，并为神经合成的定向控制策略提供了依据，表明编码特征随层深变化且中间层具联合编码优势。

Abstract: Neural audio synthesis models like the Realtime Audio Variational autoEncoder (RAVE) achieve impressive genera tion quality, yet how their internal representations encode musical features remains poorly understood. We present a systematic layer-wise and cross-layer cluster analysis of RAVE decoder activations across three models trained on different musical domains, tested with four stimulus types. We then evaluate architectural generalization with a general purpose EnCodec model. For RAVE, we find that synthetic stimuli are encoded well across models and audio features (pitch |\r{ho}|=0.45, 5.1x the null, BPM |\r{ho}| = 0.76, 8.6x the null). These results are reduced but still substantively apparent when using natural audio (mean across features |\r{ho}|=0.25, 2.8x the null). Natural audio sees a stronger encoding when nonlinear probes are used (mean across features R2=0.56, 18x the null, +0.152 nonlinear gain over the linear probe R2). Encoding strength varies throughout the layers of the decoder and an increased ability to joint-encode in the middle layers is seen across all audio features (\b{eta}2 all negative, p < 0.05). The general purpose EnCodec decoder also sees similar strong synthetic responses across audio features, similar nonlinear gains for natural audio joint encoding and similar depth profiles. We find the best cross-layer cluster improves the strength (r = 0.65, p = 0.006) and prevalence (r = 0.75, p = 0.001) of BPM encoding when compared against the best whole layers within the same section, with no effect for joint encoding. These findings advance the interpretability of neural audio models and inform targeted control strategies for neural synthesis.

</details>


### [82] [Exploiting Acoustic and Content-Oriented Speaker Verification Attacks Against Multilingual Voice Anonymization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08107)
*Ridwan Arefeen,Ze Li,Rong Tong,Ming Li,Xiaoxiao Miao*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文研究多语言匿名语音上的攻击者ASV系统，考察声学型与内容型攻击者，并构建多语言语音转换数据集以提升跨语言泛化能力，指出攻击效果取决于匿名语音的语言可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 攻击者ASV研究此前主要集中于英语，其在多语言场景下的行为尚不明确；而传统ASV已表明声学信息与上下文信息对多语言说话人验证均很重要，因此需要探究匿名语音下是否同样如此。

Method: 在多语言匿名语音上分别评估声学导向与内容导向的攻击者，并构建一个多语言语音转换（MultiVC）数据集以改善跨语言泛化。

Result: 攻击者有效性取决于匿名语音的语言可用性；总体上声学导向攻击者表现更优，但当语言信息保留良好时，内容导向与声学导向攻击者的性能差距较语音失真较强的情形有所缩小；多语言语音转换数据集进一步提升了性能并部分缩小了跨语言差距。

Conclusion: 需要更全面的攻击者建模与评测协议，同时考虑隐私与可用性，而非依赖单一攻击策略。

Abstract: Attacker ASV systems for voice anonymization have been studied primarily in English, leaving their behavior in multilingual settings largely unexplored. Conventional ASV has shown that both acoustic and contextual information are important for multilingual speaker verification. Inspired by this, we investigate whether the same holds for attacker ASV on anonymized speech. We evaluate both acoustic- and content-oriented attackers on multilingual anonymized speech and construct a multilingual voice-converted dataset to improve cross-lingual generalization. Our results show that attacker effectiveness depends on the linguistic utility of the anonymized speech. Overall, acoustic-oriented attackers achieve better performance. However, when linguistic information is well preserved, the performance gap between content- and acoustic-oriented attackers narrows compared with conditions involving stronger speech distortion. The multilingual voice-converted dataset further improves performance and partially reduces the cross-lingual gap. These findings highlight the need for more comprehensive attacker modeling and evaluation protocols that consider both privacy and utility, rather than relying on a attacker strategy\footnote{Full code and pretrained models and MultiVC Dataset link are available at: https://github.com/monkeyDarefeen/DAST

</details>


### [83] [Geometric Representations for Transformed Pattern Matching in Music](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08236)
*David Meredith*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文回顾了用点集表示音乐的方法，论证其在无词复调音乐（如键盘音乐）的模式匹配上优于序列表示；提出八种音高-时间几何表示类型，可建模移调、倒影、增值、减值、逆行等音乐变换，并引入变换类与模式间变换图刻画拉威尔《以海顿之名的小步舞曲》中海顿主题各出现之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列表示在处理无词、复调音乐（如键盘音乐）时，难以有效匹配其中的音乐模式；需要一种更适合这类音乐结构的表示方式，并能统一刻画多种音乐变换。

Method: 采用点集表示音乐，用音高-时间平面上的几何变换（平移、沿时间轴缩放、沿时间轴反射）建模移调、倒影、减值、增值、逆行等变换；按音高表示（半音音高、形态音高、morph、chroma）与时间表示（起始时间、中间时间）组合，识别出八类几何音高-时间表示；定义变换类并考察 $F_{\mathrm{2STR}}$、$F_{\mathrm{2STRMod7}}$、$F_{\mathrm{2STRMod12}}$ 三类；引入模式集 $S$ 与变换类 $F$ 的模式间变换图，顶点为模式，边表示可由 $F$ 中变换相互映射。

Result: 不同表示类型可刻画不同音乐变换：用中间时间替代起始时间可精确刻画某些逆行关系；用 morph 与 chroma 音高表示可刻画涉及八度移位与重复的变换。借助模式间变换图，拉威尔《以海顿之名的小步舞曲》中海顿主题各出现之间的音乐关系，可在所考虑的音高-时间表示中，用 $F_{\mathrm{2STR}}$、$F_{\mathrm{2STRMod7}}$ 和 $F_{\mathrm{2STRMod12}}$ 中的变换精确描述。

Conclusion: 点集式音高-时间几何表示比序列表示更适合无词复调音乐的模式匹配；通过组合音高与时间表示类型以及定义变换类，可系统刻画移调、倒影、逆行、八度移位等关系，并以变换图精确描述具体作品中主题出现之间的音乐关联。

Abstract: We review the notion of representing music using point sets and argue that such representations are better adapted than sequential representations for matching patterns in unvoiced, polyphonic music, such as keyboard music. Musical transformations such as transposition, inversion, diminution, augmentation and retrograde can be modelled by geometric transformations in pitch-time representations that combine translation with scaling parallel to and reflection in the time axis. We identify eight types of geometric pitch-time representation that use chromatic pitch, morphetic pitch, morph or chroma to represent pitch and either onset time or midtime to represent time. We illustrate how these types of representation allow us to characterise different types of musical transformation. For example, by using midtime instead of onset time, we can precisely characterise certain retrograde relationships; and by using morph and chroma pitch representations we can characterise transformations involving octave displacements and duplications. We present the concept of a transformation class and consider the three specific classes, $F_{\mathrm{2STR}}$, $F_{\mathrm{2STRMod7}}$ and $F_{\mathrm{2STRMod12}}$. We introduce the notion of an inter-pattern transformation graph for a set of patterns, $S$, and a transformation class, $F$. Each vertex in such a graph represents a pattern in $S$ and there is an edge in the graph from pattern $P_1$ to pattern $P_2$ if and only if $P_1$ can be mapped onto $P_2$ by a transformation in $F$. We show, with the aid of such graphs, that the musical relationships between the occurrences of the HAYDN theme in Ravel's Menuet sur le nom d'Haydn can be precisely described in terms of transformations in $F_{\mathrm{2STR}}$, $F_{\mathrm{2STRMod7}}$ and $F_{\mathrm{2STRMod12}}$ within the pitch-time representations considered.

</details>


### [84] [WorldSonus: Bringing Sound to Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08760)
*Pengjun Fang,Jingyi Fa,Kam Man Wu,Jiaming Wang,Haoyuan Huang,Yaguang Wu,Xiangjun Huang,Ziyang Ma,Weijia Chen,Hongyu Liu,Zeyue Tian,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: WorldSonus 是一个面向世界模型的交互式视频转音频框架，实现实时、可交互、空间对齐的立体声合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型生成的视觉环境日益逼真，但大多缺乏声音；为世界模型引入声音面临三大挑战：实时生成以匹配交互式视频流、交互控制以响应生成中的声音指令、空间对齐的立体声以反映场景几何与相机运动。

Method: 采用流式因果自回归扩散架构实现实时生成；引入以音频为中心的描述生成流程配合分块索引提示调度，实现生成过程中的动态声音事件操控；利用从多样立体声与环绕声数据中整理的高质量立体声监督实现空间对齐。

Result: 合成音频分块的实时因子（RTF）低至 0.41；在开放域视频转音频基准上有效泛化，在声学质量与空间对齐方面匹配或超越当前最先进的双向模型。

Conclusion: WorldSonus 虽为世界模型定制，但能有效泛化至开放域视频转音频任务，兼顾实时性、交互性与空间对齐，展现了良好的通用性。

Abstract: Recent advances in world models have enabled increasingly realistic visual synthesis. However, these generated environments remain largely silent. Bringing sound to world models poses three core challenges: real-time generation to keep pace with interactive video streams, interactive control to respond to mid-stream sound instructions, and spatially aligned stereo to reflect scene geometry and camera motion. To address these demands, we introduce WorldSonus, an interactive video-to-audio framework designed for real-time spatial sound synthesis in world models. For real-time generation, WorldSonus employs a streaming causal autoregressive diffusion architecture that synthesizes audio chunks at a low real-time factor (RTF) of 0.41. For interactive control, we incorporate an audio-centric captioning pipeline with chunk-indexed prompt scheduling, enabling dynamic manipulation of sound events during generation. For spatial alignment, we leverage high-quality stereo supervision curated from diverse stereo and ambisonic data. Extensive experiments demonstrate that while tailored for world models, WorldSonus generalizes effectively to open-domain video-to-audio benchmarks, matching or outperforming state-of-the-art bidirectional models in both acoustic quality and spatial alignment. Project page: https://noizai.github.io/WorldSonus/

</details>


### [85] [Restore, Separate, Restore: A Modular Framework for Music Source Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08284)
*Tobias Morocutti,Emmanouil Karystinaios,Gerhard Widmer*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出音乐源修复（MSR）任务，并给出“先修复混合、再分离、最后分轨专家修复”的三阶段框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统源分离假设混合是干净源的线性叠加，无法逆转录制过程中的均衡、压缩等非线性制作效果以及编解码等传输退化，因此需要 MSR 来恢复原始未处理的音轨。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 混合修复模型，在分离前先修复退化；(2) 单一模型将修复后的混合分离为八类目标音轨（人声、吉他、键盘、合成器、贝斯、鼓、打击乐、管弦乐）；(3) 针对分离器自身残留伪影微调的分轨特定修复专家。

Result: 在 MSR Challenge 测试集上，每个阶段都比前一阶段提升修复质量。

Conclusion: MSR 区别于传统源分离，需同时处理非线性制作与传输退化；所提三阶段框架可逐步提升修复效果，并已开源代码与模型以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Music Source Restoration (MSR) seeks to recover original, unprocessed instrument stems from mixed, mastered, and possibly degraded recordings. Unlike conventional source separation, which treats the mixture as a linear sum of clean sources, MSR must additionally invert nonlinear production effects, such as equalization and compression, and transmission-related degradations, such as codec artifacts. We propose a three-stage framework built around this distinction: (1) a mixture restoration model that addresses degradation before separation, (2) a single model that separates the restored mixture into eight target stems (vocals, guitars, keyboards, synthesizers, bass, drums, percussion, and orchestra), and (3) stem-specific restoration experts fine-tuned on the separator's own residual artifacts. Each stage improves restoration quality over the previous one on the MSR Challenge test set. We release code and models to support future research in MSR at https://github.com/theMoro/music_source_restoration.

</details>


### [86] [Sobolev Norms in Neural Embeddings Measure Audio Morphing Regularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08295)
*Théo Chasle Cauchy,Modan Tailleur,Barbara Pascal,Fanny Roche,Mathieu Lagrange*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出SDIM（Sobolev Distances to Ideal Morphing），一种在感知相关的音频嵌入空间中量化音频变形轨迹规则性的客观指标，并验证其区分能力优于现有指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频变形（morphing）在音乐声音合成中可生成两个目标之间的中间声音，帮助音乐人和声音工程师探索具有有趣感知特性的新声音。但变形本质上以感知方式定义，使得该任务的评估十分困难，现有方法缺乏可靠的客观评价手段。

Method: 提出SDIM（Sobolev Distances to Ideal Morphing）指标，在感知相关的音频嵌入空间中衡量变形轨迹的规则性。借助基于物理的合成器，在具有不同规则程度的受控变形轨迹上评估SDIM的判别能力，并与现有音频变形指标进行比较。

Result: 实验结果表明，与当前最先进的指标相反，所提指标能够可靠地将理想轨迹与对抗性轨迹区分开来。

Conclusion: SDIM为音频变形提供了一种有效的客观评价指标，在受控实验中展现出优于现有方法的判别力，有望用于音乐声音合成中的变形质量评估。

Abstract: Morphing has recently gained renewed interest with the emergence of generative models, particularly in audio and image generation. In musical sound synthesis, morphing can generate intermediate sounds between two targets, helping musicians and sound engineers explore new sounds with interesting perceptual properties. As morphing is inherently defined in perceptual terms, evaluating this task is challenging. In this work, we introduce Sobolev Distances to Ideal Morphing (SDIM), a novel objective metric to quantify the regularity of audio morphing trajectories in perceptually relevant audio embedding spaces. Leveraging a physics-based sound synthesizer, we evaluate the discriminative power of SDIM on controlled morphing trajectories with varying degrees of regularity and compare it with that of existing audio morphing metrics. Results show that, contrary to state-of-the-art metrics, the proposed metric reliably discriminates desirable trajectories from adversarial ones.

</details>

### [87] [Statistical Turbulence and High-Fidelity Disturbance Fields for Quadrotor Flight Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06874)
*Xun Huang*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文对PPO四旋翼控制器进行跨保真度训练-测试评估，覆盖从无风、离散阵风、统计湍流、合成相干结构到大气边界层大涡模拟五种风场保真度，并与PID和SE(3)控制器对比。结果表明训练-测试矩阵高度扁平，最廉价的结构化风（离散阵风域随机化）在各测试列均排名第一，控制权限而非风场真实性决定鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习四旋翼控制器通常在简化风模型下训练，但风场保真度（而非风速大小）对策略鲁棒性的影响尚未被量化。作者旨在系统厘清风场保真度投资的必要性与边界。

Method: 构建五级扰动保真度：无风飞行、离散1-cosine阵风、统计湍流、合成相干结构、大气边界层大涡模拟场。在0–12 m/s风速扫描下，对PPO智能体进行全跨保真度训练-测试矩阵评估，并以级联PID与几何SE(3)控制器作为免训练基线。所有扰动数据在使用前均经定量校验：合成生成器对照解析解或认证标准，大涡模拟场对照对数律。

Result: 在竞赛级四旋翼悬停任务上，训练-测试矩阵异常扁平，最廉价的结构化训练风（离散阵风域随机化）在每个测试列中均排名第一，该排名在风敏的27 g平台上得到复现；经一次整定的几何控制器以零坠机率包络了所学PPO策略的表现。

Conclusion: 机制诊断表明限制鲁棒性的是控制权限而非风场真实性，因此风场保真度投入应与平台的风敏感性成比例缩放，而非盲目追求高保真风场。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning quadrotor controllers are usually trained under simplified wind models, yet the impact of wind-field fidelity, as opposed to magnitude, on policy robustness remains unquantified. This paper compares five disturbance-fidelity levels, from wind-free flight and discrete 1-cosine gusts through statistical turbulence and synthetic coherent structures to large-eddy-simulation fields of the atmospheric boundary layer, in a full cross-fidelity train test evaluation of proximal policy optimization (PPO) agents, with cascaded PID and geometric SE(3) controllers as training-free references, over a 0-12 m/s wind sweep. Before any controller comparison is made, all disturbance data are validated: every synthetic generator is checked quantitatively against its analytical or certification-standard reference, and the large-eddy-simulation fields against the imposed log law. On a racing-class quadrotor in hover, the train test matrix is remarkably flat, and the cheapest structured training wind, which is discrete-gust domain randomization, ranks first in every test column, a ranking replicated on a wind-sensitive 27 g platform; a once-tuned geometric controller brackets the learned PPO policies at zero crash rate. Mechanism diagnostics show that control authority, not wind realism, bounds robustness, so wind-fidelity investment should scale with platform wind sensitivity.

</details>


### [88] [Impact of bubble nucleation on the functioning of the pulsating heat pipe: numerical simulation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06901)
*Vadim Nikolayev,Iaroslav Nekrashevych*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 采用自研PHP数值模拟代码CASCO，研究气泡生成（沸腾）对10匝水-铜管式脉动热管振荡特性的影响，通过改变成核壁垒ΔTnucl与热负荷Pe，获得热阻Rth随ΔTnucl的非单调变化规律及流型图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气泡生成对脉动热管（PHP）运行具有积极作用，但其对已建立振荡的具体影响机制尚不清晰，尤其是成核过热度与热负荷如何共同改变PHP的运行模式与传热性能，需要系统研究。

Method: 使用内部开发的PHP数值模拟代码CASCO，对10匝水-铜管式PHP（蒸发段各匝独立加热）进行数值模拟。在保持其他参数不变的前提下，改变成核壁垒（即发生成核的局部过热度ΔTnucl）与热负荷Pe，并在水平与垂直有利两种朝向下，以热阻Rth评价传热性能，进而分析运行模式并绘制Pe–ΔTnucl流型图。

Result: Rth(ΔTnucl)关系并非单调：在高ΔTnucl时热阻随ΔTnucl增大而单调升高，符合预期；而在小ΔTnucl时Rth随ΔTnucl减小而降低，因而出现极小值。该极小值源于稳定PHP运行模式之间的转变，不同模式在液塞进入蒸发段的平均穿透深度上差异显著。对水平PHP，这些模式分别对应间歇式与连续式振荡。最终得到以Pe–ΔTnucl为坐标的流型图。

Conclusion: 气泡生成对PHP振荡的影响受成核壁垒与热负荷共同控制：Rth(ΔTnucl)存在极小值，反映稳定运行模式间的转变；水平PHP下对应间歇与连续振荡两种模式，并可由Pe–ΔTnucl流型图刻画。

Abstract: In this communication, we discuss results obtained with the numerical in-house PHP simulation code CASCO (French abbreviation for Advanced PHP simulation code). The aim is to study the impact of bubble generation (boiling) on the established oscillation of a PHP with a high turn number (10-turn water-copper tubular PHP with independently heated turns in the evaporator region). We vary the nucleation barrier value, i.e. the local superheating $Δ$Tnucl at which the nucleation occurs and the heat load Pe; other simulation parameters are kept constant. The PHP heat transfer performance is evaluated with the PHP thermal resistance Rth for both horizontal and vertical favorable PHP orientation. Since the bubble generation plays a positive role for the PHP functioning, one expects that the function Rth($Δ$Tnucl) monotonously increases. This is true for high $Δ$Tnucl. For small $Δ$Tnucl, the Rth($Δ$Tnucl) function is decreasing, thus a minimum occurs. It appears because of a transition between the regimes of stable PHP functioning. The regimes strongly differ by the average penetration of liquid plugs into the evaporator. For horizontal PHP, these regimes correspond to the intermittent and continuous oscillations. The regimes are discussed and the regime map in the coordinates Pe -$Δ$Tnucl is obtained.

</details>


### [89] [Well-posed by Design: Learning Constitutive Laws from Velocity Data using Convex Neural Network Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07236)
*Gonzalo G. de Diego,Georg Stadler*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出ICNNE，直接学习耗散势而非本构律，通过对称化强制凸性、框架无关性与关于第二应变率不变量的偶性，并惩罚原点零梯度以保证耗散性，结合Firedrake与PyTorch以弱形式求解PDE约束反问题，在四种流动上验证了物理恢复、几何泛化与数值稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从速度场等间接观测反演复杂流体本构律是PDE约束反问题。表达力强的神经网络参数化易破坏正问题的适定性（如唯一性、稳定性与可泛化性），且标准形式在小应变率下易出现数值不稳定。作者希望将数学结构嵌入参数化本身，而非仅嵌入损失，从而同时保证适定性与泛化能力。

Method: 核心思路是学习标量耗散势而非本构律；其凸性、框架无关性与耗散性可传播至连续介质力学，保证正问题结构良好。提出ICNNE：输入凸神经网络架构，经对称化精确强制凸性、框架无关性以及关于第二应变率不变量的偶性，缓解小应变率下的数值不稳定；通过对势函数原点处零梯度施加惩罚，弱式满足耗散性。损失目标与梯度由有限元与神经网络方法联合计算，耦合Firedrake与PyTorch。

Result: 在四个涵盖可压缩与不可压缩情形的算例上评估：可压缩Navier-Stokes、Herschel-Bulkley屈服应力流、Hibler粘塑性海冰模型，以及无已知本构律的离散元法数据。结果显示：学习到的势（i）在已知真值情形下可恢复真实物理；（ii）能准确迁移至训练中未见几何；（iii）在非结构方法发散的工况下仍成功收敛。

Conclusion: 将数学结构嵌入参数化（而非损失函数），是从间接数据学习物理的稳健路径；耗散势加ICNNE的结构化建模兼顾适定性、稳定性与泛化性。

Abstract: Learning constitutive laws of complex fluids from velocity data (i.e. indirect observations) is a PDE-constrained inverse problem in which expressive neural parameterizations risk breaking the well-posedness of the forward physics model. We address this tension by learning, rather than the constitutive law itself, the dissipation potential: a scalar function whose convexity, frame-indifference, and dissipativity propagate into the underlying continuum mechanics and guarantee the structural properties needed for a well-posed and generalizable forward PDE. We introduce ICNNE, an input-convex neural architecture that exactly enforces convexity, frame-indifference, and evenness in the second strain-rate invariant via symmetrization, alleviating the numerical instabilities that occur at small strain rates in standard formulations. By weakly enforcing zero gradients in the origin of the potential via penalization, ICNNE also satisfies the dissipativity property. The loss objective and its gradient are computed using a combination of finite element and neural network methods, coupling the Firedrake and PyTorch libraries. We evaluate on four problems spanning compressible and incompressible regimes: compressible Navier-Stokes, Herschel-Bulkley yield-stress flow, Hibler's viscous-plastic sea-ice model, and discrete-element-method data with no known constitutive law. We show that the learned potentials (i) recover ground-truth physics where it is known, (ii) transfer accurately to geometries unseen during training, and (iii) succeed in regimes where unstructured methods diverge. These results suggest that embedding mathematical structure into the parameterization, rather than into the loss, is a robust path to learning physics from indirect data.

</details>


### [90] [Small density differences, large Richardson numbers: buoyancy decides where gypsum deposits in a centifluidic confluence cell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07305)
*Isaac Appelquist Løge,Candela Almazán Pérez,Maike W. Baltussen*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 小通道中两股密度差仅百分之几的流体混合通常按常密度处理；本文通过实验与模拟表明，只要理查森数不小，浮力效应不可忽略，并给出染色流股到达对侧壁的无量纲距离标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程惯例认为密度差在几个百分点以下即可忽略浮力、按常密度建模。本文检验这一假设在氯化钙与硫酸钠汇流生成石膏的confluence cell中是否成立。

Method: 对反应流进行常密度数值模拟；开展密度差0.14%–2.1%、三种通道高度、32个流量下的染色实验，测量染色流股到达对侧壁的距离；并进行密度匹配的染色与反应实验。

Result: 常密度模拟预测石膏沿通道中心线底部沉积，而实验中石膏在两侧壁呈带状生成，且流量降低时沉积带起始位置更上游。染色实验表明，到达对侧壁的距离由体积理查森数 Ri=g'H/U² 控制，满足 L/H = 3.31 Ri^(-0.66)，在四个数量级 Ri 范围内 R²=0.89。密度匹配时染色流股多数条件下不再到达对侧壁，且反应实验的石膏沉积位于底部，与常密度模拟一致。

Conclusion: 小的相对密度差并非忽略浮力的充分条件，还必须理查森数足够小；对centifluidic器件的典型流量，该条件常常不满足，因此须考虑浮力驱动的分层与二次流。

Abstract: The mixing of two aqueous streams in small channels is usually modeled with constant density when the difference in density between the streams is only a few percent or less. In this paper, we investigate whether this holds in a confluence cell where calcium chloride and sodium sulfate meet and form gypsum. A constant density simulation of the reacting flow results in deposition on the floor along the channel centreline. However, in the experiment, gypsum instead forms bands on both side walls, and the deposition bands start further upstream as the flow rate decreases. Dye experiments with density differences between the streams of 0.14 to 2.1\,\%, three channel heights, and 32 flow rates show that the distance at which the dyed stream reaches the opposite side wall is governed by the bulk Richardson number, $Ri=g'H/U^2$, and follows $L/H = 3.31\,Ri^{-0.66}$ over four decades of $Ri$ ($R^2=0.89$). When the two streams' densities match, the dyed stream no longer reaches the opposite wall under most conditions, and in a density-matched reacting experiment, gypsum deposits on the floor, as the constant-density simulation predicts. A small relative density difference is thus not a sufficient condition for neglecting buoyancy: the Richardson number must also be small. For the flow rates typical of centifluidic devices, it often is not.

</details>

*L. S. Klyachko*
