TL;DR: 本文是一篇1934年Klyachko关于流体中球体阻力公式的俄文原始文献的英译前言。作者因文献检索中难以获取该文、且发现西方文献对其引用多有失实，故翻译并公开该历史性工作，认为该公式迄今最为简洁优雅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在文献检索中，作者通过图书馆系统获取1934年Klyachko原始文献极为困难；在获得莫斯科俄罗斯图书馆提供的扫描件并阅读后，发现西方文献对该工作的引用稀少且常伴随不准确的描述和评论，说明多数西方作者仅获二手信息、未读过原文。与现当代文献中的各类关联式相比，Klyachko 1934年的球体阻力公式迄今最为优雅、简洁，故有必要翻译并公开该历史文献，便于全球英语读者获取。

Method: 通过图书馆系统及莫斯科俄罗斯图书馆获取1934年Klyachko俄文原著的扫描件，进行阅读与整理，并组织将其翻译为英文，在公开文献中发表。

Result: 成功获得该原始文献扫描件并完成识别与翻译筹备；指出西方文献中对其引用存在不准确描述与评论的问题。

Conclusion: Klyachko 1934年的流体中球体阻力公式是目前最简洁优雅的关联式；通过英译并公开发表这一历史性工作，可提升其全球可及性，纠正既有引用偏差。

Abstract: Preface for the English Translation: While checking out early USSR journal articles for our literature search, we recently had tremendous difficulty acquiring this 1934 Klyachko original publication through our library systems. After obtaining and reading a scanned copy of it supplied by the Russian library in Moscow, we realized that relatively scarce citations of this work in the Western literature are often accompanied by inaccurate descriptions and comments, indicating that most Western authors might have gotten only second-hand knowledge about it and never seemed to have read the original article. Compared with various correlation formulas in the modern literature, the Klyachko 1934 formula for sphere drag in fluid flow appears to be the most elegant and cleanest one thus far. Therefore, we decided to make an effort to translate this historical work and publish it in the open literature to facilitate its accessibility to English readers worldwide.

</details>


### [92] [(Why) Jets quantise trapped acoustic modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07414)
*Brandon Yeung,Michael N. Stavropoulos,Vincent Jaunet,Peter Jordan,Oliver T. Schmidt*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 高亚声速湍流射流的势核内存在被捕获的声模态，其频率成组分布于窄带内；本研究通过大规模纹影实验与LES结合对称性约束的SPOD，证实每个频带由约10个间隔ΔSt~0.01的离散频率组成，并给出模态在St、x、k上的量化规律及驻波共振模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证早前假设：高亚声速湍流射流势核中捕获的声模态并非连续频带，而是由多个离散频率构成；同时厘清其空间-波数结构与稳定性机制。

Method: 以Mach 0.9、Re=10^6射流为对象，采集约10^5个纹影实验与LES快照，采用正弦锥（sine tapers）并施加旋转与反射对称性的SPOD算法进行谱分解；随后用局部、全局、模态与非模态稳定性理论预测捕获模态，并构建同相驻波共振模型。

Result: SPOD结果显示捕获模态在St-x与St-k平面内有界；边界使模态在St、x、k上量子化，第nx个模态沿x和k具有nx个波腹。稳定性分析显示模态与非模态机制之间存在竞争；仅依据理论边界建立的驻波共振模型可预测量子化的全局模态频率。

Conclusion: 证实了窄带内由O(10)个离散频率组成的捕获声模态结构，揭示其量子化规律，并提出可由理论边界直接预测全局模态频率的简单驻波共振机制，同时指出模态与非模态稳定性竞争的物理图景。

Abstract: High-subsonic turbulent jets trap acoustic modes in their potential core. The modes lie in narrow frequency bands that are $O(0.1)$-wide in Strouhal number, $St$. By studying a Mach 0.9, Reynolds number $10^6$ jet, this work confirms an earlier hypothesis: each band is made up of $O(10)$ discrete frequencies, $ΔSt \sim O(0.01)$ apart. To resolve them, we collect $O(100\,000)$ snapshots from dedicated schlieren experiments and large-eddy simulations (LES), and apply spectral proper orthogonal decomposition (SPOD). Our SPOD algorithm uses sine tapers and enforces the jet's rotational and reflectional symmetries. For both the schlieren and the LES, the trapped SPOD modes are bounded in the $St$-$x$ and $St$-$k$ planes, where $x$ and $k$ are respectively the axial coordinate and wavenumber. The bounds quantise the modes in $St$, $x$, and $k$, such that the $n_x$th mode in $St$ has $n_x$ antinodes along $x$ and $k$. We then predict trapped modes with local, global, modal, and non-modal stability theories. We find evidence of a competition between modal and non-modal stability. From only the theoretical bounds, we build a simple in-phase standing-wave resonance model that predicts the quantised global-mode frequencies.

</details>


### [93] [Number Theory of Decaying Turbulence 3: Comparing with Physical and Numerical Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07433)
*Alexander Migdal,Wei Wang*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文比较Euler系综理论对自由衰减不可压缩湍流的预测（能谱标度函数、二阶结构函数指数、衰减律）与数值模拟及实验，理论在惯性区主体与高雷诺数极限下吻合良好，但红外截断与衰减指数依赖初始条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验该系列前两篇论文推导的Euler系综理论对自由衰减湍流的预测是否与直接数值模拟和风洞实验一致，特别是固定形状预测在何种条件下成立，以及衰减律的可测性问题。

Method: 将Euler系综解（形状固定，仅拟合归一化与尺度）与Rodhiya和Sreenivasan的4096³直接数值模拟、Max Planck风洞实验（Re_λ最高5779）以及Matsuzawa等人的自由衰减湍流团实验进行对比；分析谱塌缩、体尺度增长的抛物律、能谱红外截断k₀的影响及无限雷诺数外推。

Result: 在4096³模拟中，湍流吸引子由体尺度增长的抛物律选定，塌缩谱仅依赖瞬时雷诺数，且对Saffman与Loitsyansky初始谱相同；外推至无限雷诺数时理论在耗散区以下的熵范围内与预测吻合达1%，能量平衡精确成立。能量积分红外发散，衰减律E∝t^{-5/4}、Ω∝t^{-9/4}仅在截断进入k^{-7/2}尾部时达到，拟合截断位于谱主体下缘，衰减指数依赖初始红外谱。风洞实验中纵向指数外推值与单尺度参数的奇系综在大间距处吻合，标准交叉形式失效。Matsuzawa等人的湍流团因过小、不均匀且过弱无法显示吸引子。

Conclusion: Euler系综理论在谱主体区（熵范围）与高雷诺数极限下得到数值与实验支持，但衰减律的可测指数受红外截断支配，依赖初始条件；大间距标度行为支持奇系综预测；小型自由衰减湍流实验不足以检验该吸引子。可靠检验应聚焦谱主体而非全局衰减律。

Abstract: We compare the predictions of the Euler-ensemble solution of freely decaying incompressible turbulence with simulations and experiments: the scaling function of the energy spectrum, the index of the second-order structure function and the decay laws. The predictions have a fixed shape; only their normalization and scale are fitted. In the final $4096^3$ direct numerical simulations of Rodhiya and Sreenivasan, the turbulent attractor is selected by a parabola law for the growth of the bulk length. Along it the collapsed spectrum depends only on the instantaneous Reynolds number, identically for Saffman and Loitsyansky initial spectra; extrapolated to infinite Reynolds number, it agrees with the theory to 1% in the bulk of the enstrophy range, below the dissipation range. The energy balance holds exactly. The energy integral of the theory diverges in the infrared, so energy and enstrophy depend on how a finite flow cuts off the spectrum: with a sharp cutoff $k_0$, the decay laws, energy $\propto t^{-5/4}$ and enstrophy $\propto t^{-9/4}$, are reached only when the cutoff enters the $k^{-7/2}$ tail of the spectrum. Such a cutoff is only an estimate; the reliable test is the bulk of the spectrum. Fitted to the simulations, the cutoff lies at the lower edge of the bulk, where the spectrum still carries the initial data, and the decay exponents depend on the initial infrared spectrum. In the Max Planck wind tunnel at $Re_λ$ up to 5779, the longitudinal index extrapolated to infinite Reynolds number matches the odd ensemble with one scale parameter at large separations, where standard crossover forms fail. The freely decaying turbulent blob of Matsuzawa et al. is too small, too inhomogeneous and too weak to display the attractor. The theory is derived in the first two papers of this series.

</details>


### [94] [Erasing and Regenerating the Turbulent Cascade Arrow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07449)
*Yang Zhao,Daocheng Zhou,Hongfu Zhang*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 通过 Haar 分布厄米相位抹除湍流场的高阶相位信息，仅保留同模态二次信息，发现可积奇通量泛函期望为零；非线性 Navier-Stokes 演化能重建符号通量标量，线性演化与冻结对照则不能，表明级联方向箭头依赖二次记录之外的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流级联具有明确的方向性（能量正向传递），但该方向性的信息基础尚不明确：究竟是振幅、能谱等二次统计量决定级联方向，还是需要更高阶的相位信息。作者希望分离并检验"仅给定规定同模态二次信息"时，级联箭头能否被保留。

Method: 引入 Haar 分布的厄米相位对场施加随机化，在保持规定同模态二次信息（模态振幅、能谱、同模态双时刻关联）不变的前提下构造相位-Haar 系综，统计可积奇通量泛函的期望；比较有限系综下平均输运与前向事件偏置；对比非线性 Navier-Stokes、线性演化与幅值匹配冻结对照在匹配演化协议下的表现，并检验通量与局域三次几何标量是否同步恢复。

Result: 相位-Haar 系综中任何可积奇通量泛函的期望为零；有限系综显著抑制平均输运与主区块的前向事件偏置；独立区块保留了符号分离但未通过尺度-8 平均判据；在匹配演化协议下，非线性 Navier-Stokes 动力学将选定的符号通量标量恢复到接近其自然参考值，线性演化与幅值匹配冻结对照均不能；通量与局域三次几何标量同时恢复，但高局域关联可在箭头被抹除后仍存留。

Conclusion: 符号化（有向）输运需要超出所保留二次记录之外的信息，而所检验的非线性动力学能够重建这些信息；级联方向并非由同模态二次统计量唯一决定。

Abstract: Which information tells a turbulent flow which way to cascade? We apply Haar-distributed Hermitian phases that retain prescribed same-mode quadratic information, including modal amplitudes, the energy spectrum, and same-mode two-time correlations. For the phase-Haar ensemble, the expectation of any integrable odd flux functional is zero. Finite ensembles strongly suppress mean transfer and forward-event bias in the primary block. An independent block retains sign separation but fails the scale-8 mean criterion. In matched evolution protocols, nonlinear Navier-Stokes dynamics restores the selected signed-flux scalar close to its natural reference, whereas linear evolution and an amplitude-matched frozen control do not. Flux and a local cubic geometry scalar recover together, yet high local correlation can survive erasure of the arrow. Signed transfer thus requires information beyond the retained quadratic record; the tested nonlinear dynamics rebuilds it.

</details>


### [95] [Vectorial discrete unified gas kinetic scheme for continuum compressible flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07664)
*Yinghao Ma,Ziyang Xin,Zhaoli Guo*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出向量离散统一气体动理学格式（V-DUGKS）用于连续介质可压缩流动，通过向量动理学框架和二阶Hermite截断实现可调Prandtl数、紧凑离散速度集与更高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量DUGKS在连续介质可压缩流动中离散速度集较大、对数值参考温度敏感、稳定性与效率受限，需要一种更紧凑高效的动理学框架。

Method: 构建向量动理学框架：质量、动量、总能量由耦合分布函数表示，动量与能量输运分别松弛，从而可调Prandtl数；所有平衡态截断至二阶Hermite，仅需二阶速度矩即可恢复可压缩Navier-Stokes方程，故采用四阶Gauss-Hermite求积精确求矩；消除三阶Hermite项以统一使用紧凑离散速度集；采用有限体积离散、特征型通量评估与梯形碰撞积分。

Result: 通过激波管、Shu-Osher、二维Riemann及三维Taylor-Green涡等数值算例验证了精度与鲁棒性；三维Taylor-Green涡中，在相同CFL约束下因允许更大时间步与更简化的平衡态形式，实现约2.2-2.9倍加速。

Conclusion: V-DUGKS为连续介质可压缩流动模拟提供了一种鲁棒且高效的动理学框架，较标量DUGKS降低了参考温度敏感性并提升稳定性与效率。

Abstract: A vectorial discrete unified gas kinetic scheme (V-DUGKS) is proposed for continuum compressible flows. In the vectorial kinetic framework, mass, momentum, and total energy are represented by coupled distribution functions with separate relaxation processes for momentum and energy transport, enabling an adjustable Prandtl number. All equilibrium distributions are truncated at the second-order Hermite level. Since only second-order velocity moments are required to recover the compressible Navier-Stokes equations, fourth-order Gauss-Hermite quadrature is sufficient for exact moment evaluation. Eliminating third-order Hermite terms allows compact discrete velocity sets to be used uniformly for all distribution functions, reducing sensitivity to the numerical reference temperature and improving stability and efficiency over the scalar DUGKS. The scheme employs a finite-volume formulation with characteristic-based flux evaluation and trapezoidal collision integration. Numerical tests, including shock-tube, Shu-Osher, two-dimensional Riemann, and three-dimensional Taylor-Green vortex problems, demonstrate its accuracy and robustness. For the three-dimensional Taylor-Green vortex, V-DUGKS achieves a speed-up of about 2.2-2.9 under the same CFL constraint due to a larger allowable time step and simplified equilibrium formulation. These results show that V-DUGKS provides a robust and efficient kinetic framework for continuum compressible flow simulations.

</details>


### [96] [Airborne liquid marble: Evaporation dynamics of liquid marble in acoustic levitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07703)
*Keigo Noro,Xiao Ma,Koji Hasegawa*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究结合背光成像与背景纹影(BOS)技术，同步观测声悬浮液滴(LM)的形貌与蒸汽浓度场，揭示其在中等湿度下蒸发行为近似纯水液滴，而在高/低湿度下因颗粒壳层显著偏离，并伴随持续扁平化变形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液滴(LM)由疏水颗粒包裹，可实现非润湿操控与无容器处理，适合微反应器应用；与声悬浮结合可形成无接触反应平台。但声场下液滴的变形、内流与蒸发等复杂行为认识不足，尤其是蒸发动力学尚不清晰。

Method: 采用声悬浮装置承载液滴，利用背光成像获取液滴形貌，并采用背景纹影法(BOS)可视化周围蒸汽浓度场，实现形貌与浓度场的同步测量；在不同相对湿度条件下对比液滴与纯水液滴的蒸发速率、蒸汽分布及形状演化。

Result: 在中等相对湿度(RH=40%)下，液滴的蒸发速率与蒸汽分布与纯水液滴高度相似，表明颗粒壳层影响甚微；在高湿与低湿条件下二者差异显著，归因于疏水颗粒层带来的蒸发阻力与界面性质。纯水液滴基本保持准球形，而液滴随时间逐渐扁平化，体现颗粒壳层的机械约束作用。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了声悬浮液滴蒸发与变形耦合行为，深化了对声场下液滴动力学的理解，可为无污染微流控与微反应器系统设计提供指导。

Abstract: Liquid marbles (LMs), droplets encapsulated by hydrophobic particles, allow for non-wetting manipulation and containerless handling, making them well-suited for applications such as microreactors. When integrated with acoustic levitation, the LMs serve as contact-free reaction platforms. However, the complex behaviours associated with acoustic fields, including deformation, internal flow, and evaporation, remain insufficiently understood. This study investigates the evaporation dynamics of acoustically levitated LMs. Simultaneous visualisation of the droplet morphology and surrounding vapour concentration fields was achieved using backlighting and the Background-oriented Schlieren (BOS) method. At intermediate relative humidity (RH = 40%), the evaporation rates and vapour distributions of LMs closely resembled those of pure water droplets, indicating that the particle shell exerts minimal influence under these conditions. Conversely, significant differences were observed at high- and low-humidity, attributable to the evaporation resistance and interfacial properties introduced by the hydrophobic particle layers. Furthermore, although pure water droplets maintained a quasi-spherical shape, LMs demonstrated progressive flattening over time, suggesting mechanical constraints imposed by the particle shell. These findings provide new insights into the coupled evaporation and deformation behaviours of LMs in acoustic fields, informing the design of contamination-free microfluidic and microreactor systems.

</details>


### [97] [Direct numerical simulations of multi-layer Rayleigh-Taylor instability under asymmetric interfacial density stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07714)
*Abhijeet Guha,Prasoon Suchandra,Aditi Sengupta*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 采用全可压缩三维高精度数值模拟，研究界面Atwood数对多层瑞利-泰勒不稳定性演化、湍流混合与能谱特征的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层密度分层界面间的耦合不稳定性（双界面RTI）在惯性约束聚变、天体物理与地球物理流中普遍存在，但界面Atwood数如何调控不稳定性增长、界面耦合与湍流混合的规律尚不清晰，需要系统性参数研究。

Method: 使用全可压缩、三维高保真Navier-Stokes求解器，构建由温度变化驱动的三层流体、两个不稳定密度界面的构型，在宽范围界面Atwood数下模拟十个算例；通过最大涡量、混合层增长、密度脉动、湍动能及重力势能-TKE-耗散间的能量分配来刻画流动时间演化，并分析晚期能谱。

Result: 所有构型均经历起始、准指数增长与非线性饱和三个阶段；界面At随数增大导致不稳定性更早起爆、涡量放大增强、湍流混合层发展加速。弱分层情形接近单界面RTI动力学，强分层情形则出现两个同时活跃的混合层，随后相互作用并合并为耦合湍流区。随分层增强，密度脉动与TKE的壁法向分布逐渐展宽，湍流活动重分布加剧。晚期能谱在中等波数区间呈现约-11/5标度，与浮力主导的湍流输运一致。

Conclusion: 界面Atwood数是控制多层RTI耦合动力学、混合层结构与能量分配的关键参数；强分层下双混合层的耦合与合并行为及-11/5能谱标度为分层湍流混合建模提供了定量依据。

Abstract: Direct numerical simulations of thermally stratified multi-layer Rayleigh-Taylor instability are performed to investigate the influence of interfacial Atwood number (At) on instability growth, interfacial coupling, turbulent mixing, and spectral characteristics. A fully compressible, three-dimensional, high-fidelity Navier-Stokes solver has been used to simulate ten cases spanning a broad range of interfacial $At$ to examine the evolution of coupled instability dynamics. The configuration consists of three superposed fluid layers with unstable density stratification across two interfaces generated through temperature variations. The temporal evolution of the flow is characterized using the maximum vorticity, mixing-layer growth, density fluctuations, turbulent kinetic energy, and energy partitioning between gravitational potential energy, TKE, and dissipation. The instability evolution is shown to proceed through distinct onset, quasi-exponential growth, and nonlinear saturation stages for all configurations. Increasing interfacial At leads to earlier instability onset, enhanced vorticity amplification, and accelerated development of turbulent mixing layers. The weakly stratified configurations exhibit dynamics resembling effectively single-interface RTI, whereas the strongly stratified cases develop two simultaneously active mixing layers that subsequently interact and merge into a coupled turbulent region. The wall-normal distributions of density fluctuations and TKE demonstrate progressive broadening of the mixing region and increasing redistribution of turbulent activity with increasing stratification strength. The late-stage spectra exhibit an approximate -11/5 scaling over an intermediate range of wavenumbers, consistent with buoyancy-dominated turbulent transport.

</details>


### [98] [Only Linear Constraints Survive Coarse-Graining: Evaluating Physics-Constrained Neural Operators on Stochastically Forced Turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07811)
*Michael Groom,Rafael Oliveira*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 在粗粒化随机强迫数据上，直接在粗网格上施加PDE残差的物理信息损失会隐性引入闭合模型；本文改用滤波器可交换的线性约束（连续性、全局动量平衡），通过闭式投影施加在FNO输出上，误差较PINN与普通FNO降低多个数量级，并使湍流rollout长期有界性大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息损失将离散PDE残差加入数据项，但该做法仅在方程对所拟合场闭合时严格成立。在粗粒化、随机强迫数据上，PDE中的非线性项一般不与滤波算子交换，粗粒化场不满足原始方程，把其残差逼零等于把隐式闭合编码进学习算子。作者因此寻求只在滤波后仍严格成立的约束，以在保持物理一致性的同时避免隐式闭合。

Method: 在周期域上采用归一化、平移不变滤波时，常系数线性约束在滤波后网格上依然成立。对二维不可压Navier-Stokes方程，这类约束包括连续性与全局动量平衡。作者利用Fourier神经算子(FNO)，在其输出上施加闭式投影，精确满足这两类约束，计算复杂度为O(N log N)。此外，对非线性约束，通过在粗训练数据上拟合一个常数亚网格通量来封闭全局能量平衡。

Result: 在二维各向同性湍流上（截断使强迫完全位于截断波数之外），投影方案的连续性误差为5.0e-7，而普通FNO为0.51、物理信息FNO为0.17；全局动量平衡误差为8.6e-11，对比7.5e-4与8.3e-4。投影仅增加2%训练时间，并将448步rollout中能量保持有界的比例从0.18提升至0.87。在已解析的Kolmogorov流上，测试时优化每条轨迹的代价约为投影的3300倍，而最大散度仍高出三个数量级。用粗训练数据拟合的常数亚网格通量封闭全局能量平衡，可消除因强行施加未闭合平衡带来的20.9%能量亏损，并使所有自由运行rollout在2000步内有界。

Conclusion: 在粗粒化随机强迫数据上，应优先施加滤波后可交换的线性约束而非未闭合的PDE残差，以避免将隐式闭合注入学习算子；闭式投影以极小开销实现严格约束并显著改善长期稳定性。对非线性约束，待其未闭合项被建模后可有效施加，例如用常数亚网格通量封闭全局能量平衡。

Abstract: A physics-informed loss adds a discretised PDE residual to the data term, but is only strictly valid if the equations being enforced are closed on the fields being fitted. This fails on coarse-grained, stochastically forced data: nonlinear terms in the PDE generally do not commute with the filter, so the coarse-grained fields do not satisfy the original equations, and driving their residual to zero encodes an implicit closure into the learned operator. Under normalised, translation-invariant filtering on a periodic domain, constant-coefficient linear constraints remain valid on the filtered grid. For the 2D incompressible Navier-Stokes equations, two such constraints are continuity and global momentum balance. We enforce these exactly by applying a closed-form projection on the output of a Fourier neural operator (FNO) at $O(N \log N)$ cost. On two-dimensional isotropic turbulence, truncated so that its forcing lies entirely beyond the cutoff wavenumber, the projection achieves a continuity error of $5.0\times10^{-7}$ against 0.51 for a plain FNO and 0.17 for a physics-informed FNO, and a global momentum balance error of $8.6\times10^{-11}$ against $7.5\times10^{-4}$ and $8.3\times10^{-4}$. The projection only costs an extra 2% of training time, and takes the fraction of rollouts whose energy remains bounded at 448 steps from 0.18 to 0.87. On resolved Kolmogorov flow, test-time optimisation costs approximately 3,300$\times$ as much per trajectory as the projection, while leaving maximum divergence three orders of magnitude higher. Nonlinear constraints can also be enforced once their unclosed terms are modelled: closing the global energy balance with a constant subgrid flux, fitted from the coarse training data alone, removes the 20.9% energy deficit that enforcing the unclosed balance produces and keeps every free-running rollout bounded over 2,000 steps.

</details>


### [99] [A Lagrangian analysis of coherent structures in 2D annular Rayleigh-Bénard convection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08024)
*Luis Álamo,Jezabel Curbelo,Kathrin Padberg-Gehle*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文从拉格朗日视角研究二维环形流体层中的对流不稳定性，提出多种拉格朗日诊断量，建立了对流羽流与FTLE局部极大值曲线的对应关系，刻画了对流卷内的相干涡旋运动，并通过滑动窗口方法与Nusselt数建立联系，最后提出基于径向FTLE剖面的黏性边界层厚度判定准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对流不稳定性研究多采用欧拉视角，难以揭示全局、长寿命相干流动结构如何自组织形成。作者希望借助拉格朗日方法获得新认识，并为对流不稳定性提供新的诊断工具。

Method: 考虑内壁均匀加热、受径向重力作用的二维环形流体层；提出若干新的拉格朗日诊断量；在广泛的无量纲参数范围内开展全面拉格朗日分析；引入有限时间运动学量的滑动窗口方法；并通过径向FTLE剖面提出黏性边界层厚度的形式化判定准则，与经典欧拉方法进行验证对比。

Result: 建立了对流羽流与有限时间李雅普诺夫指数（FTLE）场局部极大值曲线之间的启发式对应关系；刻画了对流卷内与流动非零曲率相关的相干涡旋运动；通过滑动窗口方法证明了有限时间运动学量与以Nusselt数量化的径向热通量之间的直接关系，并识别出在两者上均留下痕迹的动力学转变；提出的黏性边界层厚度判定准则与经典欧拉方法结果一致。

Conclusion: 拉格朗日诊断量能够有效刻画对流不稳定性的相干结构与动力学转变，FTLE可用于识别对流羽流、相干涡旋及黏性边界层厚度，为对流系统分析提供了与欧拉方法互补的新视角。

Abstract: Convective instabilities are studied from a Lagrangian perspective in order to gain new insights into how the resulting global, long-lived coherent flow structures organize themselves. We consider a 2D annular fluid layer uniformly heated from the inside and subject to radial gravity and propose several new Lagrangian diagnostics for characterizing its convective instabilities. In particular, we perform a comprehensive Lagrangian analysis of the flow field across a wide range of dimensionless parameters, establishing a heuristic correspondence between convective plumes and curves of local maxima of Finite-Time Lyapunov Exponent (FTLE) fields. Moreover, we characterize coherent swirling motions inside convective rolls, which are associated to the non-zero curvature of the flow. By introducing a sliding window method for our finite-time kinematic measures, we demonstrate a direct relationship with radial heat flux quantified by means of the Nusselt number and identify relevant dynamical transitions that leave a trace on both. Finally, we propose a formal criterion for determining viscous boundary layer (UBL) thickness by virtue of radial FTLE profiles and validated against a classical Eulerian method.

</details>


### [100] [Motif: A Modular Finite-Volume Framework for Transient Incompressible Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08326)
*Utku Şentürk*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文介绍并验证了二维不可压缩流动求解器 Motif：采用交错网格有限体积法离散，对流用二阶 Adams–Bashforth 显式处理，扩散用 Crank–Nicolson 隐式处理，压力–速度耦合采用投影法（标准投影与 Perot (1993) 二阶近似投影），并通过多类流动算例验证其精度与守恒性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motif 被定位为教学与研究框架，需要一套足够详细、可独立复现的数值方法描述，并系统考察其面向直接数值模拟（DNS）继续发展所涉及的数值性质（离散算子、边界条件、压力时间处理、守恒性、数值耗散与色散）。

Method: 控制方程在交错笛卡尔网格上用有限体积法离散；对流项采用二阶 Adams–Bashforth 显式积分，扩散项采用 Crank–Nicolson 隐式积分，压力–速度耦合使用投影法，并比较标准投影与 Perot (1993) 的近似二阶投影两种方案。验证算例覆盖周期性、壁面约束及进出口流动，网格包括均匀网格与光滑拉伸网格，考察空间/时间收敛性、离散质量守恒、动能行为、拟涡能（enstrophy）与能谱。

Result: 结果表明显式空间离散达到了预期精度，并清晰区分了两种投影方法在时间行为上的差异；同时验证了离散质量守恒与动能、拟涡能、能谱等相关性质。

Conclusion: 该文档既提供了足以独立实现 Motif 的详细描述，也分析了与其向直接数值模拟持续发展相关的数值特性。

Abstract: This document presents the theory, implementation, and verification of Motif, a two-dimensional incompressible flow solver developed as a teaching and research framework. The governing equations are discretized using the finite-volume method on staggered Cartesian grids. Convection is treated explicitly using the second-order Adams--Bashforth method, diffusion implicitly using the Crank--Nicolson method, and pressure--velocity coupling using projection methods. Both the Standard projection and the approximate second-order projection of Perot (1993) are considered. Particular attention is given to the discrete operators, boundary conditions, temporal treatment of pressure, conservation properties, and numerical dissipation and dispersion. The implementation is verified using a sequence of periodic, wall-bounded, and inlet--outlet flow problems on uniform and smoothly stretched grids. Spatial and temporal convergence, discrete mass conservation, kinetic-energy behavior, enstrophy, and energy spectra are examined. The results demonstrate the expected accuracy of the spatial discretization and distinguish the temporal behavior of the two projection approaches. The document is intended both to describe Motif in sufficient detail for independent implementation and to examine the numerical properties relevant to its continued development toward direct numerical simulation.

</details>


### [101] [Physically Organized Latent Spaces in Unsupervised Autoencoders: Evidence from Aerodynamic Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08451)
*Renato Tognaccini,Ettore Saetta,Gianluca Iaccarino*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 自编码器仅通过重构训练，即可自发地将潜空间组织成符合物理变量与气动规律的表征，且这种组织在多次随机初始化下可定量复现；顺序训练的三维潜空间能将无黏流场与边界层效应解耦，第三维与雷诺数和气动阻力强相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究无监督自编码器能否在仅有重构目标、无物理标签监督的条件下，从不同复杂度的气动数据中自发学到具有物理意义的潜表征，并验证其可复现性。

Method: 使用复杂度递增的气动数据库（从线性解析理论到非线性分离RANS流）；采用仅以重构为目标的自动编码器；并通过顺序训练使无黏流场与边界层效应解耦，获得三维潜空间；利用交叉验证回归与仿射对齐度量分析潜坐标与物理参数、响应间的线性关系。

Result: 潜坐标的具体取值随随机初始化而变，但其底层物理组织可复现，甚至定量一致；三维潜空间中第三维与雷诺数和气动阻力强相关；物理参数与响应在学习到的坐标中被强且线性地编码，且在独立训练间保持一致。

Conclusion: 无监督自编码器可在无物理监督下自组织出符合物理规律的潜表征，具有可复现性和线性可解释性，可用于气动流场的降维与物理解耦表示。

Abstract: Using aerodynamic databases of progressively increasing complexity - from linear analytical theory to nonlinear separated RANS flows - we demonstrate that an autoencoder, trained solely by reconstruction, can spontaneously organize the latent representation according to physically meaningful variables and aerodynamic laws. Although the latent coordinates themselves vary under random initialization, the underlying physical organization is reproducible, even quantitatively. In addition, we present a three-dimensional latent space representation of the airfoil flow obtained by an unsupervised autoencoder with sequential training in which the inviscid field is decoupled from the boundary layer effects with the third latent variable strongly correlated with the Reynolds number and aerodynamic drag. Cross-validated regression and affine-alignment measures show that physical parameters and responses are strongly and linearly encoded in the learned coordinates, consistently across independent trainings.

</details>


### [102] [Simulation of Weakly Ionized Hypersonic Flows with Reactive Species Weighting Scheme in the Direct Simulation Monte Carlo Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08476)
*Takato Morimoto,Virgile Charton,Eiichiro Yamaoka,Kiyoshi Kinefuchi*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文将守恒物种权重方案从弱电离流扩展到反应流，采用分裂-合并概念处理不同权重的粒子，在单胞解离与交换反应算例中验证了质量与总能量守恒，并成功应用于OREX再入飞行条件，给出与飞行数据一致的电子数密度与离子电流预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接模拟蒙特卡洛（DSMC）方法在弱电离流中需借助守恒物种权重方案分辨电子、离子等痕量组分；然而该方案此前仅适用于非反应流，无法处理物种依赖权重的反应碰撞，因而难以预测高超声速再入时通信黑障起始所需的电子数密度。

Method: 将守恒物种权重方案扩展至反应流：对物种依赖权重的粒子采用分裂-合并（split-merge）概念；不同权重的物种间反应碰撞按其各自权重处理，产物粒子取整数个数，并对反应物与产物粒子施加概率性处理以满足质量守恒。随后在单胞解离与交换反应算例中进行验证，并将方法应用于OREX飞行条件（105.0 km至92.8 km）。

Result: 单胞算例中验证了质量与总能量守恒，摩尔分数历程与传统DSMC一致。在OREX条件下，平动与内能温度与传统DSMC结果吻合；获得了连续的痕量电子数密度空间分布，与飞行数据中观测到的通信黑障起始一致；计算的离子电流再现了飞行静电探针数据的总体趋势。量化了壁面离子复合与N+O缔合电离速率不确定性的影响，计算电流范围涵盖了可用的测量值；105.0 km算例外推预测与总体趋势一致。

Conclusion: 该扩展方案可在反应流中保持质量与总能量守恒，并有效分辨痕量带电组分，为高超声速再入通信黑障预测提供电子数密度与离子电流，且与飞行数据吻合良好。

Abstract: The conservative species weighting scheme enables the direct simulation Monte Carlo (DSMC) method to resolve trace species such as electrons and ions in weakly ionized flows. The present study extends this scheme to reacting flows, utilizing a split-merge concept for particles with species-dependent weights. Reactive collisions between differently weighted species are processed according to their respective weights, introducing the production of an integer number of product particles and a probabilistic approach applied to both reactant and product particles to satisfy mass conservation. The extended scheme was validated in single-cell dissociation and exchange reaction test cases, verifying mass and total energy conservation and the mole-fraction histories against conventional DSMC. This extension provides the electron number density required for predicting the onset of communication blackout at high altitudes during hypersonic reentry. The method was applied to the Orbital Re-entry Experiment (OREX) flight conditions from 105.0 km to 92.8 km altitude. Translational and internal temperatures showed good agreement with conventional DSMC results, validating the macroscopic flow-field computation. The scheme enabled continuous spatial distributions of trace electron number density, consistent with the communication blackout onset observed in the flight data. The computed ion current reproduced the overall trend of the in-flight electrostatic probe data. The influences of the ion recombination at the vehicle surface and of the uncertainty of the N + O associative-ionization rate were quantified, and the range of the computed currents encompassed the available measurements. The 105.0 km case extended the prediction beyond available measurements, consistent with the overall trend.

</details>


### [103] [Thermocapillary stabilization of Liquid Space Telescopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08654)
*Ryan Engle,Valeri Frumkin*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该论文研究利用空间均匀、时间调制的加热方式，在微重力条件下稳定薄液体镜面。周期性加热产生振荡热毛细流动，抑制持续加热所引发的不稳定性。通过多尺度分析导出了表面温度演化方程，线性稳定性分析给出了稳定性判据与衰减时间，数值模拟验证了米级表面变形可在数天内得到稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在微重力环境下，薄膜液体镜面易受持续加热引起的热毛细不稳定性影响，难以维持平整自由表面；作者试图寻找一种主动控制手段来抑制该不稳定性，从而稳定液体镜面。

Method: 基于长波模型，采用多尺度分析处理多个加热周期内的耦合表面温度演化；随后开展线性稳定性分析，推导各表面模态的稳定性判据与衰减时间；并通过数值模拟进行验证。

Result: 周期性加热产生的振荡热毛细流动能抑制持续加热导致的不稳定性；分析得到了稳定性判据和模态衰减时间；数值模拟与解析预测一致，并表明米级尺度的表面变形可在数天时间尺度上实现稳定。

Conclusion: 空间均匀的时间调制加热可作为微重力下稳定薄液体镜面的有效机制，其效果源于振荡热毛细流动，并结合热辐射、热毛细输运与毛细作用使自由表面趋于平整；理论预测经数值验证，具备在米级尺度上实际应用的潜力。

Abstract: We investigate how spatially uniform, time-modulated heating can be used to stabilize a thin liquid mirror in microgravity. Periodic heating generates oscillatory thermocapillary flows that suppress the instability associated with sustained heating, allowing the free surface to relax through the combined effects of thermal radiation, thermocapillary transport, and capillarity. Using multiple-scale analysis of a long-wave model, we derive the coupled surface-temperature evolution over many heating cycles. A subsequent linear stability analysis yields the stability criteria and decay times of individual surface modes. Numerical simulations validate the analytical predictions and demonstrate stabilization of meter-scale surface deformations over timescales of days.

</details>


<div id='eess.AS'></div>

# eess.AS [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [GIVE-KWS: Gated Injection of Visual Evidence for Noise-Robust Query-by-Example Keyword Spotting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07046)
*Ming-Hsiang Hu,Kuan-Tang Huang,Hung-Shin Lee,Berlin Chen*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出 GIVE-KWS，用门控交叉注意力将唇动视觉证据注入查询音频，发现在具备音素信息的视觉表征下，注入式融合比掩码式融合在 -10 dB 下等效提升 4.0-9.3 dB SNR，未见关键词 EER 相对基线降低 72.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语音有望实现噪声鲁棒的关键词识别，但视觉流并非总被有效利用。在三模态 QbyE-KWS 基准上，任务训练的视觉编码器系统在 -10 dB 下与文本-音频系统的 EER 仍差约 2 个百分点，作者将这一差距归因于编码器缺乏音素信息。

Method: 提出 GIVE-KWS，其融合阶段 GIVE（Gated Injection of Visual Evidence）通过门控交叉注意力，用唇部运动对查询音频进行条件化。核心论点：视觉鲁棒性依赖两个相互作用的条件——携带音素的视觉表征，以及以“注入视觉证据”而非“重新缩放音频特征”的方式融合。

Result: 在携带音素的编码器下，注入式融合相比掩码式融合在 -10 dB 下带来 4.0-9.3 dB 的等效 SNR 增益；而在音素贫乏的编码器下该增益几乎消失。相对基准系统，GIVE-KWS 在 -10 dB 下将未见关键词 EER 降低 72.9%，平均降低 62.8%。

Conclusion: 视觉模态的有效利用取决于音素信息丰富的视觉表征与注入式融合机制的协同作用，二者缺一不可；GIVE 融合策略在该基准上显著提升了噪声条件下的关键词识别性能。

Abstract: Visual speech promises noise-robust keyword spotting, yet a visual stream is not necessarily used. On a tri-modal query-by-example keyword spotting (QbyE-KWS) benchmark, we find that a system with a task-trained visual encoder comes within 2 percentage points of a text-and-audio system in equal error rate (EER) at -10 dB, and link this gap to the encoder's lack of phonemic information. We present GIVE-KWS, whose fusion stage, GIVE (Gated Injection of Visual Evidence), conditions query audio on lip motion through gated cross-attention. We show that visual robustness depends on two interacting conditions: a phoneme-bearing visual representation, and fusion that injects visual evidence rather than rescaling audio features. Under a phoneme-bearing encoder, injection yields an effective SNR gain of 4.0-9.3 dB over masking at -10 dB, whereas under a phoneme-poor one it nearly vanishes. Relative to the benchmark system, GIVE-KWS reduces unseen-keyword EER by 72.9% at -10 dB and 62.8% on average.

</details>


### [105] [SEAL: Mixture-Closed Additive Reconstruction and Refinement-Aware Expert Routing for Efficient Speech Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07047)
*Shao-Chun Hu,Zi-Xiang Lin,Jeih-Weih Hung,Hung-Shin Lee*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: SEAL：用零和加性残差替代乘性掩码，用稀疏专家路由替代共享单元，实现高效紧凑的时频分离器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有紧凑时频分离器存在两大局限：一是有界乘性掩码只能对混合信号频点做缩放，当重叠分量相互抵消时估计值仍偏小；二是共享单元对所有时频 token 在每步施加相同权重，扩大单元会全面增加计算量。

Method: 提出SEAL（Sparse Expert routing with Additive Latent reconstruction）。重建方面，采用受局部混合幅度约束的零和加性残差，使分量抵消处估计可非零同时仍能求和还原混合信号。路由方面，由声学证据与步间证据构建查询，将每个 token 路由到六个残差专家之一，并用范数上限防止步线索盖过清晰的声学证据。

Result: 在EchoSet上，SEAL(small)较TIGER(small)提升0.31 dB SI-SDRi，参数少28%，MACs少2.9倍；SEAL(large)与TIGER(large)的SI-SDRi差距在0.07 dB以内，MACs少3.1倍。

Conclusion: SEAL通过加性残差重建与稀疏专家路由，同时突破了乘性掩码的表达瓶颈与共享单元的计算开销瓶颈，在更少参数和显著更低计算量下取得更优或相当的分离性能。

Abstract: Compact time-frequency separators that mask the mixture and refine through a shared cell face two limits. First, a bounded multiplicative mask only scales a mixture bin, so where overlapping components cancel, the estimate stays small. Second, a shared cell applies the same weights to every time-frequency token at every step, so enlarging it adds compute everywhere. We present SEAL (Sparse Expert routing with Additive Latent reconstruction) to address both. For reconstruction, a zero-sum additive residual bounded by the local mixture amplitude lets estimates be nonzero where components cancel yet still sum to the mixture. For routing, a query built from acoustic and inter-step evidence sends each token to one of six residual experts, and a norm cap keeps the step cue from overriding clear acoustic evidence. On EchoSet, SEAL (small) surpasses TIGER (small) by 0.31 dB SI-SDRi with 28% fewer parameters and 2.9 times fewer MACs, and SEAL (large) is within 0.07 dB SI-SDRi of TIGER (large) at 3.1 times fewer MACs.

</details>


### [106] [Logbook: Extremely Long-form Audio Event Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07338)
*Kwanghee Choi,Suwon Shon,Dmitriy Serdyuk,Guitang Lan,Chao-Wei Huang,Mohammad Sadegh Rasooli,Sangeeta Srivastava,Zhaojiang Lin,Saurabh Adya,Ming Sun*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出Logbook——一个面向小时级音频理解的基准，录音时长从10分钟到6天。任务要求系统对连续音频进行无缝分段，并为每段给出事件标签与描述。作者比较了52个端到端与级联系统，并消融了微调、上下文长度与推理预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频基准大多基于短小、预先切分好的片段，导致模型设计局限于短输入或固定词表，无法评估长时程、连续音频的真实理解能力。

Method: 构建Logbook基准，包含10分钟至6天的连续录音；给定录音与事件标签词表，要求输出无缝（gap-free）分段，每段附带事件标签和描述；对比52个端到端及级联系统，并对微调、上下文长度、推理预算进行消融实验。

Result: 任务整体可解，但最优系统仍低于人类参考水平；过分割（over-segmentation）现象普遍，微调可在一定程度上缓解；端到端系统通常优于级联系统，但在更长上下文下性能下降。

Conclusion: Logbook填补了小时级连续音频理解的评测空白，揭示了过分割与长上下文退化等关键问题，为长时程音频理解模型的未来改进提供了方向与基线。

Abstract: Audio benchmarks are built around short, pre-segmented clips, limiting model design to brief inputs or fixed vocabularies. To close this gap, we introduce Logbook, a benchmark for hour-scale audio understanding, with recordings ranging from ten minutes to six days. Given a continuous audio recording and an event label vocabulary, a system must predict a gap-free segmentation with an event label and a description per segment. We compare 52 systems, end-to-end and cascaded, and ablate fine-tuning, context length, and reasoning budget. We find the task tractable, though the best systems remain below the human reference. Also, over-segmentation is pervasive, and fine-tuning partially mitigates it. Finally, end-to-end are often better than cascaded systems, but degrades with longer context.

</details>


### [107] [Region-Aware Masking for Accent-Robust Cross-Lingual Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07524)
*Haoqi Li,Shivam Mehta,Ravi Teja Gadde,Yinghong Lan*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 通过调整掩码几何位置，使训练与推理的跨语言提示模式一致，从而显著降低跨语言零样本TTS中的口音泄漏，且不损失可懂度与自然度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言零样本TTS存在口音泄漏问题，模型会把说话人音色与源语言口音一并迁移到目标语言输出中。在掩码重建式TTS中，该问题因训练—推理不匹配而被放大：训练时基于同语言上下文进行重建，推理时却依赖跨语言上下文。

Method: 将两种语言的语句拼接，并相对于语言边界放置重建掩码，使训练阶段呈现与推理一致的跨语言提示模式。该方法无需语言ID、口音标签、同说话人平行录音或架构改动，仅改变掩码几何。

Result: 母语者听测中，区域感知掩码将感知口音从4.3降至0.5（0–5量表）；相对仅双语适配，在已见情形降低18–51%，在未见源语言降低35–55%；可懂度与自然度无显著损失。比较掩码几何发现，位置而非掩码量决定口音抑制与说话人保持的平衡：仅限重建区域的掩码口音抑制最强但说话人保持最不可靠，双区域掩码则可两者兼顾。

Conclusion: 掩码几何是提升口音鲁棒性的简单而有效的调节手段，也为跨语言零样本TTS的性能—自然度权衡提供了新思路。

Abstract: Accent leakage remains a critical challenge in cross-lingual zero-shot text-to-speech (TTS), where models inadvertently transfer both the speaker's timbre and source-language accent into the target-language output. In mask-reconstruction TTS this is amplified by a train--inference mismatch: training reconstructs from same-language context, while inference conditions on cross-lingual context. We close this gap by concatenating utterances from two languages and placing the reconstruction mask relative to the language boundary, so training presents the cross-lingual prompting pattern the model encounters at inference. This requires no language IDs, accent labels, parallel same-speaker recordings, or architectural changes---only the mask geometry differs. In a listening study with native speakers, region-aware masking reduces perceived accent from 4.3 to 0.5 on a 0-5 scale against the unadapted baseline, and by 18-51\% in seen regimes and 35-55\% on unseen source languages against bilingual adaptation alone, with no measurable loss in intelligibility or naturalness. Comparing mask geometries shows that placement, not the amount masked, governs the balance between accent suppression and speaker preservation: masks confined to the reconstruction region suppress accent most but retain the reference speaker least reliably, whereas two-region masking achieves both. Mask geometry is thus a simple, effective lever for accent robustness.

</details>


### [108] [A Novel Sentence Stress Detection Framework Leveraging Auxiliary Word-Stress Modeling and Loss Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07626)
*Tien-Hong Lo,Fong-Chun Tsai,Ting-An Hung,Yu-Hsuan Hsieh,Yao-Ting Sung,Berlin Chen*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 该论文针对自动发音评估中句子重音检测(SSD)与词重音检测(WSD)相互独立建模的问题，提出将SSD与WSD联合建模的新范式，并引入词跨度重音正则项(WSR)，在TinyStress-15K基准上取得最优SSD性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSD识别承载语义的显著词、WSD确定词内主重音音节，二者共享音高、时长、强度等韵律线索，但以往工作多将两者视为独立任务，忽视了其共享的韵律信息，存在建模割裂的不足。

Method: 提出一种有效的SSD方法，通过新颖的建模范式将SSD与辅助WSD任务联合建模；同时设计词跨度重音正则项(WSR)，将token级的SSD概率约束聚焦于每个重音词的跨度内。

Result: 在TinyStress-15K基准上的实验表明，所提方法优于多个强基线，且完整配置取得最佳SSD结果。

Conclusion: 联合建模SSD与WSD并配合WSR正则化可有效提升句子重音检测性能，验证了共享韵律线索建模的合理性与有效性。

Abstract: Prosodic stress is a crucial aspect of automatic pronunciation assessment (APA), encompassing both sentence stress detection (SSD) and word stress detection (WSD). SSD highlights semantically salient words that shape discourse meaning, while WSD identifies the primary stressed syllable within each word to ensure lexical clarity. However, most prior work treats SSD and WSD as independent tasks, overlooking their shared reliance on prosodic cues such as pitch, duration, and intensity. To address this gap, we propose an effective SSD approach combining SSD with auxiliary WSD via a novel modeling paradigm. In addition, we introduce a word-span stress regularizer (WSR) that concentrates token-level SSD probabilities within each stressed word span. Experiments on the TinyStress-15K benchmark show that the proposed method outperforms strong baselines, with the complete configuration achieving the best SSD result.

</details>


### [109] [HINTT Submission to the 2nd MLC-SLM Challenge: Comparing Cascaded and Unified Approaches to Diarization and ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08063)
*Takanori Ashihara,Kohei Matsuura,Masato Mimura*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文介绍参加 MLC-SLM 挑战赛 Task 1 的 HINTT 系统，面向多语言说话人属性 ASR，比较级联式（说话人日志+语音LLM ASR）与统一式语音LLM 两种建模策略，最终提交采用级联方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言说话人属性 ASR 需同时确定“谁在何时说了什么”，面临级联式与统一式两类建模路线的选择问题，且需在无外部数据与伪标签约束下验证哪种策略更可靠。

Method: 设计并对比两种策略：(1) 级联流水线，微调 DiariZen 说话人日志 + 微调 Qwen3-ASR + 基于 LLM 的生成式纠错；(2) 统一语音 LLM，直接输出说话人标签、时间戳与转写，并微调 VibeVoice-ASR。所有任务相关微调与模型选择仅使用官方 MLC-SLM 数据。

Result: 在 MLC-SLM Task 1 条件下，级联系统表现更稳定可靠；统一语音 LLM 则展现出面向未来说话人属性 ASR 的潜力。

Conclusion: 当前任务中，含日志、ASR 与生成式纠错的级联方案优于统一建模；统一语音 LLM 是有前景的后续方向。

Abstract: This paper presents the HINTT system submitted to the 2nd Challenge and Workshop on Multilingual Conversational Speech Language Model (MLC-SLM). We address multilingual speaker-attributed ASR, where systems must determine who spoke when and what was spoken. We investigate two modeling strategies for this problem: a cascaded pipeline that combines speaker diarization with speech-LLM-based ASR, and a unified speech LLM that directly generates speaker labels, timestamps, and transcriptions. Our final submission is based on the cascaded pipeline, consisting of a fine-tuned DiariZen diarization model, a fine-tuned Qwen3-ASR model, and LLM-based generative error correction. For comparison, we also fine-tune VibeVoice-ASR as a unified model using the same official training data. All task-specific fine-tuning and model selection are performed using only the official MLC-SLM data, without external data or pseudo-labels. Experimental results demonstrate that the cascaded system remains more reliable under the MLC-SLM Task 1 conditions, while unified speech LLMs offer a promising direction for future speaker-attributed ASR.

</details>


### [110] [Conversation Is a Two-Body Problem: Dyadic Evaluation of Full-Duplex Dialogue Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08125)
*Sungnyun Kim,Sungwoo Cho,Jihwan Oh,Se-Young Yun*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 论文提出 DyaFDB，一个以"双人对话"方式评估全双工语音对话模型的框架：让两个模型在指定角色与目标下直接对话，并由外部裁判对双方离线打分，从而检验轮流、重叠与打断等双向耦合行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全双工口语对话模型普遍面对"单侧对话者"进行评估：要么是预录音频（无法反应），要么是能实时反应但只执行固定测试序列、且自身不被评分的自动考官。这类单侧框架只能评估"双体问题"的一半，而轮流、重叠、打断本质上是两个耦合说话者共同产生的现象，因此无法刻画模型在真实双向互动中的行为。

Method: 提出 DyaFDB 框架，采用双人（dyadic）设置：两个全双工模型按分配的角色直接对话，目标可合作也可冲突，双方均由外部裁判离线评分。论文实例化 4 类任务、共 140 个场景，覆盖 6 种自博弈与交叉博弈配对，共录制 7,560 段对话；并公开场景、角色提示与双模型录制协议，且不使用任何预录音频。

Result: 共录制 7,560 段对话，覆盖 4 个任务、140 个场景与 6 种自博弈/交叉博弈配对。实验观察到模型的行为会持续重塑其对话伙伴，即伙伴的表现随对方行为而动态变化。

Conclusion: 每个模型必须同时充当对方的考官与被考者，任何单一固定对话者都无法胜任这两个角色；双人互评是评估全双工对话模型更恰当的范式，为轮流、重叠与打断等耦合行为提供了可复现的评测基础。

Abstract: Full-duplex spoken dialogue models listen and speak at the same time, enabling voice agents to have natural, low-latency interactions that turn-based systems cannot offer. However, they are commonly evaluated against single-sided interlocutors: pre-recorded audio that cannot react, or an automated examiner that reacts in real time but only administers a fixed sequence of tests and is never graded. These single-sided frameworks evaluate only half of a two-body problem, where turn-taking, overlap, and interruption are joint products of two coupled speakers. We propose DyaFDB, a framework that evaluates full-duplex models in a dyadic setup: two models converse directly under assigned roles with cooperative or conflicting goals, and both sides are scored offline with an external judge. DyaFDB probes how the two models behave toward each other, such as how they take turns or carry an assigned role under different interests. We instantiate four tasks as 140 scenarios and record 7,560 conversations, covering six self- and cross-play pairings. Throughout the experiments, we observe that how a model behaves continually reshapes its partner. We thus demonstrate that each model must be both the examiner and examinee of the other, and no single fixed interlocutor can play both parts. We will release the scenarios, role prompts, and recording protocols between two full-duplex models, without any pre-recorded audio.

</details>


### [111] [CTAG-FX: Reinterpreting Synthesizer Parameter Spaces for Expressive Tone-Shaping Audio FX Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08182)
*Geonung Jo,Jongeun Choi*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出CTAG-FX，将文本条件合成器的78个参数重新解释为音色塑形效果器的控制空间，实现可复用的FX处理器，并通过信号分析与映射打乱消融验证角色映射的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导音频效果研究多聚焦于将自然语言描述映射为已有效果系统的参数或链路配置，而较少关注单个效果处理器内部控制空间本身如何构建。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索合成器参数空间作为新音频效果控制空间设计资源的可能性。

Method: 提出CTAG-FX，将文本条件合成器配置中的78个参数功能性重解释为音色塑形效果器的控制参数。每个合成器配置定义一个固定的音色塑形处理器，可重复应用于新音频输入，而非仅生成一次性渲染输出。文本条件合成器配置由A&R-CTAG生成，这是CTAG的检索增强扩展。评估采用信号级分析与映射打乱消融（随机置换参数到控制的对应关系）来检验角色分配贡献。

Result: 基于角色的映射产生具有提示区分度的频谱与非线性行为；打乱映射会削弱这种提示特定差异，得到对提示更不敏感的非线性特征。消融结果支持了角色分配的有效性。

Conclusion: 结果表明合成器参数空间不仅可作为声音生成空间，还可作为构建新音频效果控制空间的设计资源。音频样例见项目页面。

Abstract: Recent text-guided audio FX research has focused on translating natural-language descriptions into parameters or chain configurations within existing FX systems. However, comparatively little attention has been paid to how the internal control space of an individual FX processor can itself be constructed. To address this gap, we propose CTAG-FX, which functionally reinterprets the roles of 78 parameters in a text-conditioned synthesizer configuration as controls of a tone-shaping FX processor. Each synthesizer configuration defines a fixed tone-shaping processor that can be repeatedly applied to new audio inputs rather than producing only a one-off rendered output. The text-conditioned synthesizer configurations are generated using A&R-CTAG, a retrieval-enhanced extension of CTAG. We evaluate CTAG-FX through signal-level analysis, together with a scrambled-mapping ablation in which parameter-to-control assignments are randomly permuted to assess the contribution of the proposed role assignment. Role-based mapping produces prompt-distinct spectral and nonlinear behavior, whereas scrambling reduces this prompt-specific differentiation and yields a more prompt-insensitive nonlinear profile. Overall, these results suggest that synthesizer parameter spaces can serve not only as sound-generation spaces but also as design resources for constructing new audio-FX control spaces. Audio samples are available at https://taylor3527eg-hub.github.io/ctag-fx-platform-demo/.

</details>


### [112] [Voice Anonymization Made Simple: Training-Free Anonymization with Projected Classifier-Free Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08276)
*Xiang Shi,Han Zhu,Ming Li,Xiaoxiao Miao*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练的两阶段语音匿名化方法，用于预训练流匹配语音转换系统，仅修改推理阶段的条件与引导，提升说话人隐私同时保持内容与情感信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音匿名化方法多需专门训练，成本高且泛化受限；本文希望利用预训练流匹配语音转换系统，在不更新任何参数的前提下实现匿名化。

Method: 两阶段方法：1）条件构建阶段，在随机选取的说话人上下文下重新生成源内容表示，以减少残留源说话人信息并保留语言内容；2）语音生成阶段，用源语音确定与原说话人相关的生成方向，抑制源对齐分量同时保留有用的内容引导。仅修改推理时的条件与引导，不更新预训练参数。

Result: 在 VoicePrivacy 2026 Track 1 上，两阶段方法提升了说话人隐私，同时保持有竞争力的词错误率与情感识别性能。

Conclusion: 免训练、仅推理阶段干预的两阶段策略可在流匹配语音转换框架下有效实现语音匿名化，兼顾隐私保护与语言/副语言信息保留。

Abstract: Voice anonymization aims to conceal speaker identity while preserving linguistic and paralinguistic information, yet many existing methods require dedicated training. We propose a training-free, two-stage anonymization method for pretrained flow-matching voice conversion systems. In the condition-construction stage, the source content representation is regenerated under a randomly selected speaker context to reduce residual source-speaker information while preserving linguistic content. In the speech-generation stage, the source speech is used to identify the generation direction associated with the original speaker, and the source-aligned component is suppressed while useful content guidance is retained. The proposed method modifies only inference-time conditioning and guidance, without updating any pretrained parameters. Experiments on VoicePrivacy 2026 Track 1 show that the two-stage method improves speaker privacy while maintaining competitive word error rate and emotion recognition performance.

</details>


### [113] [Audiovisual joint learning for end-to-end hearing aids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08579)
*You-Jin Li,Yu Tsao,Borching Su,Kuan-Chung Ting,Fan-Gang Zeng*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出AV-NeuroAMP，一种端到端视听框架，联合含噪语音、目标说话人视频与听者听力图，同时完成语音增强、个性化放大与动态范围压缩，并引入听力图条件特征调制模块AC-FiLM，在客观与主观评测中均优于传统及两阶段方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 助听器用户在噪声（尤其他人同时说话）下言语理解困难。传统助听器将语音增强与听力损失补偿分为独立阶段，增强误差与信号失真会传递到放大阶段；同时纯音频语音增强在竞争说话人条件下收益有限，因目标与干扰语音声学特征相近而难以分离，因此需引入视听信息并实现端到端联合处理。

Method: 提出AV-NeuroAMP端到端视听框架，输入含噪语音、目标说话人视频及听者听力图，联合执行语音增强、个性化放大和动态范围压缩；并设计听力图条件特征线性调制（AC-FiLM），将听者特异性听力曲线有效融入模型。

Result: 客观评测显示，AV-NeuroAMP在域内英语测试集上优于传统放大、纯音频NeuroAMP模型及两阶段系统，且在未见过的普通话测试集上保持改进。听音测试中，模拟听力损失的正常听力被试与真实听力损失听者均报告语音质量与可懂度提升，竞争说话人条件下收益最大。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，端到端视听个性化放大是提升助听器在困难声学环境下性能的有前景方向。

Abstract: Speech understanding in noise remains challenging for hearing-aid users, particularly in the presence of competing speakers. Conventional hearing aids typically perform speech enhancement (SE) and hearing-loss compensation in separate stages, which may cause enhancement errors and signal distortions to carry over to the amplification stage. Moreover, audio-only SE often provides limited benefits under competing-speech conditions because the target and interfering speech share similar acoustic characteristics, making them difficult to separate. To address these limitations, we propose AV-NeuroAMP, an end-to-end audiovisual framework that integrates noisy speech, target-talker video, and the listener's audiogram to jointly perform SE, personalized amplification, and dynamic-range compression. We further introduce audiogram-conditioned feature-wise linear modulation (AC-FiLM) to effectively incorporate listener-specific hearing profiles. Objective evaluations showed that AV-NeuroAMP outperformed conventional amplification, the audio-only NeuroAMP model, and two-stage systems on an in-domain English test set, and that these improvements were retained on an unseen Mandarin test set. Listening tests involving normal-hearing participants under simulated hearing loss and listeners with hearing loss further demonstrated improvements in speech quality and intelligibility, with the greatest benefits observed under competing-speech conditions. These findings support end-to-end audiovisual personalized amplification as a promising approach for improving hearing-aid performance in challenging acoustic environments.

</details>
